The navmesh itself still stops short of the deck (1 m > the 0.5 m step): a ramp or beach up to deck height would join it.

### Lumora Outskirts is now the Terrain3D zone (2026-09-22)
- SCENES SWAPPED: the Terrain3D rebuild is now `Scenes/lumora_outskirts3d.tscn` (it keeps its own scene uid, uid://bdunec8ddde6y) and the original flat zone is archived as `Scenes/lumora_outskirts3d_flat.tscn` (uid://bpou2mfjpik5n). Renamed rather than repointed because the file name is used as the zone key: Data/compass.json looks up "lumora_outskirts3d", and world_announcer.gd announces the zone from it ("Lumora Outskirts"). So Global.START_ZONE_PATH, Net.pending_zone_path, the music map and the dedicated server all load the terrain zone with no other change.
- Tools: tools/bake_lumora_navmesh.gd now bakes Lumora Outskirts (the terrain zone) by default and the archived flat zone with `-- flat`; tools/regen_lumora_collision.gd/.sh only apply to the archived flat zone. The navmesh files keep their names (terrain zone: Data/lumora_outskirts_terrain_navmesh.tres).
- Verified headless on the renamed zone: the crafting test passes; the terrain test passes apart from its two known false alarms (its NPC ground ray starts 3 m up and hits the stall roofs above the vendors; the dock deck is off the navmesh by design — the merchant hops it); a dedicated server starts on it, makes the 19 structures solid, places the 10 stations / 82 nodes, and its own world check reports ground under the spawn and a working navmesh. Terrain3D logs "Cannot find the active camera" once on a headless server — harmless with collision_mode FULL_GAME (collision is built at load; the camera only drives LOD).

### Test 17 fixes: crash, lag, loot window, dock and water (2026-09-22)
- CRASH "Invalid type in function 'target_key_of' ... (previously freed)" (player3d.gd _process): TargetFrame.target_key_of() and target_of() did check is_instance_valid(), but their parameter was typed Node, and Godot rejects a FREED object at the call — before the check can run — so a mob that died and despawned while targeted crashed the game. Both now take an untyped parameter; player3d.gd also drops a freed current_target each frame.
- LAG on the Terrain3D zone: measured headless (30 s, same character, ~60 mobs): 606 frames vs 4,349 on the flat zone. Not the terrain collision or the building collision (removing either changed nothing) and not the mob count — the mobs' PATHFINDING: every AI state set nav_agent.target_position every physics frame, and NavigationAgent3D re-plans the whole path on every assignment even to the same point, so 60 full path searches a frame; cheap on the flat zone's 391-polygon navmesh, heavy on the terrain's ~7,000. monster3d.gd now sets it through _set_nav_target(), which only re-targets when the point moved > 1 m (a patrol point is planned once, a chased player about every metre). Also removed a leftover per-frame debug print ("CHASE: target ="). After: 4,343 frames (worst frame 36 -> 20 ms); a mob 35-39 m away still chases and starts attacking in ~6 s.
- LOOT WINDOW OVERLAP: rows (icon, long name, Loot/Ignore/Sell, Take) can be ~444 px wide, over the old 420 px width cap, and after the player had ever dragged/resized the window it stopped fitting itself at all. corpse_loot_window.gd now always grows to the widest row and to MAX_LIST_HEIGHT of rows (then scrolls), keeps the player's position and any size they made bigger, and stays on screen.
- DOCK AND WATER TO GROUND LEVEL: the sea terrain was flat at 0 (level with the land), so the water plane had nowhere to sit. Carved a seabed: 0 at the shoreline (z -1022), sloping to -3 m over 15 m under the whole water plane; water surface lowered from 0.54 to -0.2 (ground level without z-fighting the shore); the dock deck lowered from 1.0 to 0 — flush with the ground, so the navmesh now reaches the Dock marker (path ends 1 m away; was 28 m) and the merchant walks on without hopping. Navmesh rebaked (7,301 polygons).
- Verified headless: terrain test and crafting test both ALL PASSED on Lumora Outskirts, with no script errors in the run.

### Gathering and crafting station models (2026-09-22)
- The user's models replace the placeholder shapes: all 10 gathering nodes (desert thistle, sand root, venom cactus flower, moonpetal sage plant, tin / copper node, uncut gem deposit, fir / ironwood / palm tree) and 7 of the 10 town stations (oven, alchemy, brewing vat, woodworking, fletching, jewelcrafting, tailor's tables). Forge, tinkering bench and tannery keep the placeholder block until they have models.
- New hand-edited `Data/crafting_models.json`: model, rotation and scale per node / station; crafting_world_spawner.gd passes each entry to gathering_node.gd / crafting_station.gd, and CraftingStation.add_model() instances the model, applies the rotation/scale and lifts it so its lowest point sits on the ground; the name label goes just above the model's top.
- ORIENTATION: every one of these .glb files has a "world" node carrying Blender's Z-up->Y-up correction. The gathering models are upright as exported (rotation [0,0,0]). The 7 tables were exported UPSIDE DOWN (their correction is +90 on X instead of -90 — e.g. the oven's "top" piece sits below its "base"), so they got [180,0,0] — then the user re-exported all 10 station models the right way up (plus the forge, tinkering bench and tanning station), so every entry is now [0,0,0]. The signpost, wagon crash and large skull (placed by hand) are fine as exported. Measured in game: fir 8.6 m, ironwood 6.4, palm 5.7, ore/plants 0.8-1.4, tables 0.9-1.7, all on the ground.

### Dedicated server couldn't load the Terrain3D zone (2026-09-22)
- Symptom: joining the dedicated server hung at "connecting" (hosting from a client worked). Server log: "Cannot get class 'Terrain3DMaterial'", "Failed loading resource ... lumora_outskirts3d.scn", then "World check: ground under spawn MISSING, navmesh BROKEN".
- Cause: the "Linux Server" export preset's exclude_filter contained addons/terrain_3d/* (from before the zone used Terrain3D), so the server build had no Terrain3D; the client presets include it. Removed it from the filter — the server export now also writes libterrain.linux.release.x86_64.so next to the binary.
- tools/update.sh uploaded only the server binary; it now also uploads the .so files next to it (and warns if none are there).
- Verified: exported the Linux Server preset and ran the build (its own user-data folder, port 8997): it loads the zone, 23 structures solid, 10 stations / 82 nodes, "World check: ground under spawn OK, navmesh OK".

### Test 19 (part 1): lost items, hills, Sahren, ocean sound, server stalls, crafting, quests (2026-09-23)
- VANISHING ITEMS (the 10 Tanning Oil): moving a bag to another basic slot left its contents keyed to the old slot, so they were orphaned. inventory_autoload.gd _swap_basic_slots now moves each bag's contents with the bag. The orphan rescue now merges into existing stacks, then fills any bag or basic slot with room; if everything is full it says so once ("N items at the bottom of your pack need room") and retries on every inventory change. Zozuur's server save still holds the orphaned Traveler's Pack contents (12 Tanning Oil, a rat tail, a Viper Poison recipe): they come back as soon as a slot is free.
- J / L IN PATCHED BUILDS: a patch can't change project.godot's input map, so Global registers toggle_quest_journal (J) and toggle_recipe_book (L) at startup when they're missing.
- WALKING THROUGH HILLS: every raised terrain point (3,643 checked) has collision, and the player lands on and walks up the new hills — a player walking through them was running a build older than the saved terrain. What was really unreliable: Godot Physics can miss a HeightMapShape with a perfectly VERTICAL ray (1,259 of 22,302 spots). New Global.ground_ray() falls back to a small swept sphere (0 misses, exact height); used by the player's login ground-lift, vendors' floor_y_at, the crafting node spawner, the server's world check and the rain-cover probe.
- SAHREN STUCK: the navmesh was baked before the latest terrain edits (rebaked: 10,012 polygons). tools/update.sh now rebakes it automatically when anything in zones/lumora_terrain/ or the zone scene is newer than the navmesh (commit the .tres afterwards). His (and the guards') obstacle ray treated a hillside rising ahead as a wall; walkable slopes (normal.y >= 0.6) no longer count. Verified: a full visit walks spawn -> dock -> gate with no stalls and no "could not reach" teleport.
- OCEAN SOUND AT THE GATE: ambient_water_sound.gd treated every mesh named "*water*" as the sea — including the forge model's "quench_water" trough 20 m from the gate. Water meshes under 400 m² are now ignored.
- SERVER "LONG FRAME 0.5 s" WITH ONE PLAYER: with nobody online monsters skip their AI, but with one player EVERY monster on the map wandered and pathed each frame: 64 mobs = ~12 ms of physics per frame on a Ryzen; the server VM (generic "QEMU Virtual CPU", 44% CPU idle vs 13% here) falls behind and Godot runs several catch-up physics steps in one frame. monster3d.gd: an idle/patrolling monster more than 120 m from every player is dormant (they notice players at 5 m at most; gate raiders keep marching); idle mobs standing on the ground skip move_and_slide; nameplate/visibility updates never run on a dedicated server. Measured with a local dedicated server and a bot client: physics 12 -> 3.5 ms per frame. The server VM would also gain a lot from exposing the host CPU (CPU type "host" instead of the QEMU default).
- NAMEPLATES only show within 20 m: monsters and other players by distance to your player (monsters refresh 5x a second instead of every frame), NPC/vendor/guard/pet labels via Label3D visibility_range_end = 20.
- INNATE RECIPES need skill 0 (Crafting.xlsx: all 20 innate recipes that needed 1-3 now 0; gathering tiers unchanged) — Tin Ingot etc. can be made by a brand-new character. Re-exported.
- DISCOVERED RECIPES: the first successful craft of any recipe logs "✦ Zozuur has discovered the recipe for Tin Ingot!" and counts it (player_data "crafted_recipes"); the recipe book shows "crafted N×" / "not yet discovered" on every row, "N of M known recipes discovered", and a "Discovered only" filter.
- QUESTS IN THE JOURNAL: picking up an auto-start quest's item now starts it (A Warden Not at Rest — found the medal; The Silent Den / Weavemother's Mark / The Wreck also start from the item), respecting chains (the Weavemother waits for The Silent Den, and starts on completing it if you already carry the sigil). With the wagon note, Sahren and the guards' topics, all 5 quests reach the journal. Verified headless.

### Test 19 (part 2): drop & destroy, ground pouches, bank, crafting bags, player trading (2026-09-23)
- DROP / DESTROY: drag an item out of a bag window onto the world (no NPC under the cursor — dropping on an NPC still offers it to them) and the whole stack drops at your feet in a leather pouch; the right-click item menu also has Drop and Destroy (Destroy asks first). New Scripts/world_items.gd (a WorldItems node in the zone scene): the SERVER keeps the list of pouches and tells every peer to show them, a pickup is granted to exactly one player (right-click within 3 m; the claim is range-checked), late joiners get the pouches already lying around, pouches vanish after 15 minutes, and a full pack puts the pouch straight back down. Inventory.take_from_slot() removes an entry only if it still holds the expected item; a bag with things in it can't be dropped or destroyed. A picked-up item is re-made from its definition (like loot).
- BANK: Oswin Coinwright <Keeper of the Lumora Vault> at the east end of the vendor row (84, -21.4): right-click or hail him (or say "bank"/"deposit") to open the bank — 24 slots per character (Inventory.bank_storage, saved with the character). Drag between the bank and your bags / character-sheet slots either way (same items stack; swaps work; a bag must be empty to bank it); bank items can only be inspected; the window closes when you walk away. New Scripts/banker_npc.gd (a talking vendor that opens the bank instead of a shop), Scripts/bank_window.gd, Data/banker.json.
- CRAFTING BAGS: every portable kit is also an 8-slot bag (in a character-sheet slot) that only holds its craft's materials — the ingredients of every recipe that kit can make, worked out from tradeskill_recipes.json at startup (e.g. the leatherworking kit: raw hide, tanning oil, soft leather, thread, fir planks, tin ingot, sandroot, hardened leather). New Large Crafting Bag (items.json, 16 slots, "bag_accepts": "crafting" — any recipe ingredient or gathered material, 62 items; Tobble sells it for 3 silver). New crafting materials go straight into a crafting bag that takes them; restricted bags are respected by every move, swap and the orphan rescue ("The Basic Tailoring Kit only holds tailoring materials."). The character sheet shows "holds tailoring materials only" on each such bag; items can now be dragged into an empty slot of a particular bag there. Kit descriptions say so (old saved kits pick up the new text).
- TRADING: right-click another player (or /trade, /trade <name>) to ask; they get Trade/Decline (30 s). The trade window: drag items from your bags to your side (a stack asks how many, up to 8 different items), click one to take it back, set platinum/gold/silver/copper; the other side shows theirs; any change clears both Accepts. When both accept, the server has each side verify it still has what it offered, can afford its coin and has room (Inventory.can_fit_all(), worked out on a copy) before telling both to swap — so a full pack or a sold item cancels nothing but the Accepts. Moving more than 12 m apart, closing the window or logging off cancels. The dedicated server logs every completed trade. New Scripts/trade_relay.gd (created by WorldItems at runtime on every peer) and Scripts/trade_window.gd; /trade listed in the pause menu's commands.
- Why new nodes and not Net: the Net autoload's RPC list must stay exactly what released builds have (it carries the version handshake), like GMRelay.
- NAVMESH UID: rebaking from a script dropped the navmesh file's uid (the scene refers to it by uid); restored, and tools/bake_lumora_navmesh.gd now puts the old uid back after saving.
- Verified: single-player drop -> pouch -> pickup -> destroy; the bank (deposit, withdraw, slot count, closes when you walk away); crafting bag rules; can_fit_all with a full pack; and on a local dedicated server with two headless players: A's pouch seen and picked up by B, then a trade (a sword + 25 copper for 2 tin ore) completed on both sides with the server's trade log line.

### Test 20: bags in the bank, trade window sizing, crafting-window leftovers (2026-09-23)
- BAGS IN THE BANK work like bags on the character sheet: a bag in a bank slot opens its own slots under the bank grid ("Traveler's Pack — 3 / 8"), a FULL bag can be deposited or withdrawn whole (its contents travel with it, in bank_storage["bags"]), items drag in and out of bank bags, and a kit / Large Crafting Bag keeps its material rules in the bank. inventory_autoload.gd's bank moves are now one routine (_move_any) for character-sheet slots, bags, bank slots and bank-bag slots; a bag with things in it still can't go inside another bag.
- TRADE WINDOW cut off / overlapping: every code-built window (GameWindow: trade, bank, quest journal, recipe book) now grows to fit its contents whenever they change, and stays on screen. The trade window's coin boxes are two labelled rows (Platinum/Gold, Silver/Copper), and offered stacks show their count in the tile's corner instead of as button text that pushed the icon aside.
- CRAFTING WINDOW LEFTOVERS: closing the window returns whatever is still in the ingredient slots to your bags. It didn't when the window closed mid-batch some other way (walking off, logging out), and a full pack lost the items; now it always returns them, and anything that doesn't fit is set down at your feet in a pouch.
- Verified headless: full bag / kit into and out of the bank with contents, items into a bank bag, the kit refusing other materials in the bank; trade and bank windows fit their contents on a 1280x720 screen.

### Test 21: Deposit / Withdraw, Sort, version 0.4.5 (2026-09-23)
- VERSION 0.4.5 (project.godot config/version). A project.godot change can't ship in a patch: publish this with `tools/update.sh --new-base` and share the new full build.
- DEPOSIT / WITHDRAW: while the bank window is open, right-clicking an item in your bags or character-sheet slots offers Deposit, and an item in the bank (or a bank bag) offers Withdraw — the whole stack, or a bag with everything in it. It goes onto a stack of the same item, else into a crafting bag that takes it, else a free slot, else any bag with room (Inventory.quick_bank_move() / _find_destination()).
- SORT button on the character sheet (above the bags) and the backpack (next to the search box): joins split stacks of the same item and moves every crafting material that a crafting bag will take out of the character-sheet slots and ordinary bags into it ("You tidy your pack: 3 items into your crafting bags."). Inventory.sort_pack().
- Kits before the Large Crafting Bag: when an item fits both, it now goes into its craft's kit (the more specific bag) — for new loot, Sort, Deposit/Withdraw and a trade's room check alike (Inventory.bag_rank()).
- Verified headless: Sort (split tin ore joined, tanning oil into the leatherworking kit, ore and logs into the Large Crafting Bag, rat tails left alone), Deposit of a sword and of a full kit, Withdraw back into the right bags; both Sort buttons and the Deposit button appear in the real windows.

### Test 22: gathering nodes move every day, invite popups (2026-09-23)
- GATHERING NODES RANDOMISED: each cluster's nodes used to be scattered from a fixed seed, so they were in exactly the same spots every session. crafting_world_spawner.gd now seeds the scatter with the in-game day as well: every in-game day (24 real minutes) every node moves to a new random spot inside its cluster (still never in town or on walls/rocks). The day comes from the server's clock, so every player sees the same layout; a joining client re-lays its nodes once the server's clock arrives. A node someone is gathering stays put until the next change. Verified: all 82 nodes move on a new day, the same day always gives the same layout, none in town.
- INVITE POPUPS: the trade invite now uses the group invite's popup (group_invite_popup.gd gained ask(text, accept, decline, callback) and expire()) — same dark panel and gold border instead of the plain grey dialog — and both open in the middle of the screen and can be dragged anywhere by the panel.

### Sound effects, balanced volume, spell projectiles, fall damage, legacy folder (2026-09-23)
- ASSETS: music moved to Assets/music/ (tidy lowercase names), sound effects to Assets/sounds/<ambient|combat|crafting|gathering|monsters|player|spells|ui|world>/ named by use; every scene/script reference updated. Assets/sounds/sounds.txt lists each file's use and its original download name (credits).
- ONE SOUND SYSTEM: Scripts/sfx.gd (static class Sfx): Sfx.play("weapon_hit", where), Sfx.start_loop(id, owner) / Sfx.stop(handle). Data/sounds.json lists every sound: files (one picked at random), loop, max_seconds (plays only the start, faded), start (skip leading silence), pitch (random +/- so repeats differ), positional (heard from where it happens). Everything is on the SFX bus; nothing plays on a dedicated server. Every Button clicks (hooked once from Global._ready()).
- BALANCED VOLUME: tools/balance_sounds.py measures every file (EBU R128 loudness via ffmpeg) and writes volume_db so each kind of sound sits at its target (UI -21..-27 LUFS, combat and spells about -18, loops -23..-29, ambience -33); the files themselves are untouched. Files ranged from -11 to -40 LUFS. MUSIC too: every track brought to -20 LUFS (they ranged -10 to -24; Sands of Lumora -5.4 dB, the combat track -6 dB), applied by global_background_music.gd including the combat crossfade. Re-run the tool after adding or replacing a file.
- WIRED IN: your swing (weapon or fist hit, miss whoosh, target's block, parry/riposte); monster swings at you (hurt, male/female; miss; your block/parry); spider attacks; jump; fall impact; death (male/female, on true death); footsteps walking/running while you move, the swim loop while you're in the sea, a heartbeat below 25% health; every spell by kind (heal/cure/dispel, buff, stealth, summon, elemental schools, other magic, weapon/archery skills); fizzle when concentration breaks; gathering loops (mining, woodcutting) + pickup; crafting loops per craft (forge, cooking, alchemy/brewing pour, tanning, sewing, gem tapping, fletching, woodworking chop) and success/fail per item; coin sound for buying, selling and looting coin; pickup for looted items and pouches; equip/unequip; window open/close (character sheet, abilities, journal, recipe book, trade), the bag for the backpack, the vault door for the bank; quest received/completed; level up; birds by day and crickets by night (ambient_nature.gd, made by the zone's AmbientOcean).
- FALL DAMAGE: a drop of more than 6 m costs 5% of max health per metre beyond it (a 16 m fall: half your health), with the fall-impact sound; not in the first 5 s after logging in or respawning.
- SPELL PROJECTILES: harmful ranged spells cast at an enemy (bolts, curses, charms...; 130 spells) now fly to the target as a glowing orb in their school's colour (fire orange, cold blue, lightning white, poison green, disease olive, magic purple, psychic pink, spirit teal, divine gold) with a trail and a light; archery skills shoot an arrow. The damage, effects and sound land when it arrives (Scripts/spell_projectile.gd; player3d.gd resolves the spell again on arrival). Melee skills, touch spells, taunts and self-effects mis-listed as enemy-targeted don't fly. For now only the caster sees their own bolts.
- LEGACY: every old 2D scene and script (19 scenes, 35 scripts: the 2D player, mobs, zone, missiles...) moved to legacy/ with an "-old" suffix, together with the old archive-2d/ folder (its "-OLD" files renamed "-old"). legacy/.gdignore makes Godot skip the folder entirely (no import, no classes, never exported); the server export filter names legacy/ instead of archive-2d/.
- Verified headless: each hook requests the right sound (a test drives falls, movement, low health, swings, monster swings, spells, windows, equip, quests, level up, gathering, ambience); a Fireball launched a bolt and the monster's health only dropped when it arrived; music plays at its balanced level; a dedicated server and the main menu start with no script errors.

### Test 23: unequip, bank sound, nature ambience, foraging sound (2026-09-23)
- UNEQUIP DID NOTHING: taking gear off only looked for a free character-sheet slot and quietly gave up when all 12 were full (Zozuur's are). It now falls back to any bag with room, and says so when there is nowhere to put it.
- BANK SOUND CUT SHORT: the vault recording is silent for its first 1.15 s and the sound was stopped at 1.3 s. It now starts where the sound does (the heavy clunk). Other sounds that began with dead air now skip it too: magic damage (0.6 s), stealth, heal, fall impact, both deaths, the miss whoosh.
- BIRDS AND CRICKETS were a constant loop all day / all night. Now every 25-70 s one 8-12 s burst plays from a random part of the recording, faded in and out, coming from a random direction 10-22 m away (ambient_nature.gd, Sfx.play_clip()).
- SELF-CAST SPELLS (e.g. Aura of the Shadow, Shadowlight) play their sound straight in your ears instead of as a 3D sound at your own feet, which could be faint with the camera pulled back. (Both already requested the buff sound in a headless test.)
- FORAGING SOUND: the new "espremendo" file is gathering/forage.mp3, looping while you gather plants (thistle, sandroot, venom cactus, moonpetal sage).
- Mining: only the mining loop plays (checked); the recording itself is a "mining ambience" with metal tapping in it, which is what sounds like the gem-tapping craft sound.
- MORE SOUNDS (the rest of the test 23 list), filed by use in Assets/sounds (sounds.txt lists the sources) and balanced:
  - crafting: brewing (moka pot), woodworking (sawing), tinkering (metallic work) — each craft now has its own loop.
  - spells: a whoosh as a bolt leaves the caster (spell_projectile.gd), and impacts by school — frost for cold, lightning for lightning, holy light for divine; fire/poison/disease keep the elemental impact. Buffs use the magic twinkle (the same file as stealth).
  - player: eating, drinking, potions, bandaging (yourself or a downed ally), and a splash when you step or fall into the sea.
  - world: rolls of thunder every 40-120 s while the rain is heavy (weather_manager.gd); and sounds tied to places, listed per zone in Data/sounds.json "emitters" and started by ambient_nature.gd: a crowd murmur in town, fire crackling at the forge, and the harbour's ship bell every 1-2.5 minutes (heard up to ~140 m).
  - spells/wind.mp3 is filed but not used yet.
- MINING "PLAYING TWO SOUNDS": right-clicking again while gathering started the next nearby vein as well (the busy one is skipped), so two mining loops overlapped. A right-click mid-gather now does nothing. The mining ambience stays as the mining sound.
- Emitter ranges: the forge's fire is heard within 5 m, the harbour bell within 15 m of the dock.

### Aetherfist review, elemental stances, weapon-speed auto-attack, Improved Parry, IP bans (2026-09-23)
- AETHERFIST LEVELS 1-8 REVIEWED: every ability now casts in 1.5 s. Fixed data: Flurry of Blows melee reach; Inner Focus a real self-heal (15% max health over 6 s, 60 s recast — it was flagged harmful with a 10-minute recast); Pressure Point melee, slows attack speed 15% for 15 s; Fire Strike melee, 60 hit + 25 burn over 5 s (the burn used to be 120); Improved Ki Strike -> "Ki Strike", a 45-damage strike (it upgraded a Ki Strike the class never had); Meditation 10/s group heal for 6 s; Stunning Fist 50 damage + 1 s stun (was 1 damage, 5 s); Enhanced Mend Wounds -> "Mend Wounds", 15/s group heal for 6 s; Water/Earth Strike melee; Swift Step now dashes 8 m and +15% run speed 4 s (was unbuilt); Wholeness of Body takes 15% less damage (was +5% damage); Flying Kick leaps to the target from up to 15 m; Ki Barrier 6 s.
- New engine support any spell can use: "effect_amount" (an effect's own size when it differs from the hit), "modifiers" (what a buff/debuff/snare changes), "heal_pct_max_hp", "leap", "dash_distance"; player modifiers dodge_bonus, parry_bonus, attack_speed_bonus, move_speed_bonus (and speed_slow now slows players too). The abilities book shows display names (Ki Strike, Mend Wounds, Mountain's Challenge).
- ELEMENTAL STANCES (Aetherfist, class_stances.json): Wind 1, Earth 3, Water 5, Fire 7, Void 10 — unlocking as you level (the stance bar grows), one at a time, 3 s between changes. Each has modifiers, auto-attack-only procs (Wind 5% stun 3-5 s; Water 15% heal for 30% of the hit; Fire 20% burn 3/s for 4 s stacking 3x; Void 15% random weaken/blind/silence/mana drain/3-5 s stun), a 25% boost to its own abilities (Wind: Swift Step goes 12 m, Flying Kick; Earth: Earth Strike, Mountain's Challenge; Water: Water Strike, Mend Wounds; Fire: Fire Strike; Void: Ki Strike), a group benefit to group members within 10 m (Earth -3% damage taken, Water +1 regen, Fire +3% damage, Wind +5% move speed, Void +5 magic resist; the same element from two monks doesn't stack) and an aura everyone sees (glow + rising motes in the element's colour; current_stance is now replicated). A monster stunned by a proc can't be proc-stunned again for 15 s. The old level-1 Wind Stance ability is retired (removed from existing Aetherfists; new ones start with Flurry of Blows).
- MOUNTAIN'S CHALLENGE (Aetherfist 3): a taunt for when the tank is down or the healer is being hit — 15 m, 20 s recast; holds the target harder in Earth Stance. Scroll sold by Ilsabet.
- AUTO-ATTACK SPEED comes from the weapon now: its "delay" in tenths of a second (dagger 2.2 s, rusty sword 2.8 s), bare hands 2.0 s for an Aetherfist and 3.0 s for others, shortened by attack_speed_bonus. It used to be the attack animation's length (swings every 0.3-1 s), so melee damage per second is now much lower than before — watch the balance in testing.
- IMPROVED PARRY works (a passive for whoever learns it: Lightsworn 3, Aetherfist 7): a parry strikes back for 5% of the damage the blow would have done ("You parry and strike back..."); relayed to the real monster on a dedicated server.
- SERVER LOG names players and shows their IP: "Peer ... connected from <ip>", "Zozuur logged in — peer ..., <ip>", "Zozuur disconnected — peer ..., <ip> (0/6)".
- IP BANS: GMs can /ban <ip | player name> (bans the address and disconnects everyone on it), /unban <ip>, /bans. Kept in the server's user://banned_ips (one address per line with a note; editable by hand, read fresh each time; --banned-ips-file= to move it). A banned address is refused on connect.
- Scrolls: new Scroll of Mountain's Challenge; the Ki Strike and Mend Wounds scrolls renamed. Data/player_spells.json is the spell source of truth (Data/player_spells.xlsx is an older copy, last saved 2026-09-16 with the old "Zenblade" class name).
- Verified headless: every changed ability (Flying Kick leaps 10 m -> 1.4 m, Fire Strike 25 burn, Pressure Point slow, Inner Focus, Wholeness, Ki Barrier 150, Swift Step 8 m), stance unlocks by level, each stance's dodge/parry/attack speed, proc stun + immunity, burn stacking to 3, the ban -> kick -> refused-on-reconnect -> unban cycle on a local server.

### Aetherfist follow-ups and every class's buffs (2026-09-23)
- AETHERFIST: Focused Strike (level 1) — a 15-minute self-buff, +10% damage and +2% hit; every Aetherfist starts with it (existing characters are given it on login). Earth Strike now interrupts (cancels a spell being cast, holds back the next attack 1.5 s). Striking Serpent (level 17) — a melee poison strike, 60 damage + venom 5/s for 8 s that stacks 3 times ("stacks" works on any DoT spell). Ki Tempest (level 19) became Dragon Fist — 15 s: +20% Double Attack chance, +10% Triple Attack chance, every auto-attack hit weakens the target (-5% damage); 90 s recast. Scrolls for Focused Strike, Striking Serpent and Dragon Fist (Ilsabet).
- BUFF DURATIONS (all classes): almost every buff had a placeholder 5-second duration. Long self/group buffs now last 15 minutes (Bless, Barkskin, Arcane Intellect, Clarity, Enchanted Clarity, Invisibility, Detect Magic, Arcane Armor, Dark Pact, Illusory Veil, Windfury, Arcane Ward, Death's Echo, Focused Strike, Aura of the Shadow); in-combat buffs and damage shields 15 seconds (about 70 of them). Descriptions say so. Kept short on purpose (too strong at 15 s): invulnerability (Divine Sanctuary, Celestial Aegis), untargetable (Sanctuary), 50-60% damage reduction (Light's Bastion, Lich's Grasp, Dark Sovereign, Ironclad Bastion), Unstable Rift, Camouflage Mastery, Serenity.
- 34 SELF-BUFFS WERE AIMED AT ENEMIES (Holy Aegis, Iron Resolve, Steel Aegis, Spirit Walk, Perfect Balance, Ki Aegis, Windrider, Shadow Cloak, Bone Armor, Mana Barrier, Ice Barrier, Entropic Ward...) — now self-targeted. Buffs that had no effect now read theirs from the description: damage shields ("absorbs 200"), damage reduction, magic resistance, dodge/evasion, movement/attack speed, damage, accuracy, mana regen; new crit_bonus modifier (Divine Favor, Light's Call, Perfect Balance, Chaos Weave); Shadow/Umbral Cloak make you invisible. Damage shields now reach other group members on their own machines (they were only set on the caster's copy).
- Then modelled the special parts too:
  - Windfury: 15% chance for an auto-attack to be followed by a second full swing ("extra_attack_chance").
  - Spirit Link: 30% of each blow the linked ally takes is spread over the rest of the group within 30 m (each member's own machine takes their share); with nobody near, it stays on the ally ("damage_share").
  - Illusions: Mirror Image 2, Arcane Mirage 3, Illusory Double 1 (+30% less damage taken) — each takes one blow that would have landed ("decoy_charges"); see-through copies of your character stand beside you, vanishing as they're struck.
  - CC immunity ("cc_immune": Unyielding Darkness, Zen Focus, Veil of Dreams, Wild Ascension, Eternal Spirit) blocks stuns, fear, charm, mesmerize, roots, slows, silences and blinds; Spirit Walk ("movement_immune") blocks roots and slows. Perfect Balance's knockback immunity is a flag only — nothing knocks back yet.
  - Shields that strike back ("reflect_pct", "reflect_chance", "retaliate_flat"): Holy Aegis 10%, Thorn Shield 15%, Ki Aegis 10%, Celestial Aegis 20%, Unstable Ward (10% chance of 20%), Umbral Fortress 30 per hit; Ice Barrier slows attackers, Chaos Shield has a 15% chance to slow/silence/blind the attacker. Shields keep these alongside their absorb.
  - Detect Magic lets you see invisible/hidden enemies for 15 minutes; Death's Echo (15 minutes) now empowers the hit after every kill instead of ending on the first.
  - Invulnerability (Divine Sanctuary, Celestial Aegis) actually blocks damage; Sanctuary hides the ally from monsters for 4 s; Zen Focus and Eternal Spirit fixed (Eternal Spirit: +40% damage, 25% less damage taken, CC immune for 15 s).
  - Still not modelled: reflecting spells back (Spell Ward, Prismatic Veil — monsters don't cast spells yet) and shields that explode when they break (Wild Barrier, Entropic Ward/Aegis).
- Verified headless: Focused Strike 900 s, Ki Barrier/Divine Ward shields, Perfect Balance crit 25 -> 40, Shadow Cloak invisibility, Dragon Fist modifiers + weaken on hit, Striking Serpent 3 stacks, Earth Strike interrupt.

### Test 25 (Shonuff, Aetherfist): icons, wording, monster health, town safety, placeable stations, loot all (2026-09-23)
- Stance icons (Assets/icons/spells/stance_wind/earth/water/fire/void.png, recoloured from existing art) on the stance bar and the buff bar. Wind's aura is pale sky blue now (Water a deeper blue so they differ). Physical self-buffs (Focused Strike, Wholeness of Body, Dragon Fist, Perfect Balance, Zen Focus, Windrider, Iron Resolve, Fortified Stance) use a gold fist icon (physical_buff.png).
- "Yourself is empowered." -> "You are empowered." (and the other self-cast effect lines); Focused Strike has its own cast line.
- Monster health: every level-1 monster now has 75 HP (the rat — "a desert scavenger" — had 20; bats and spiderlings 15), and level-2 monsters under 90 now have 90, so level 2 stays tougher.
- Badly hurt monsters that run away now limp off at 0.6x speed instead of sprinting at 1.5x.
- No monsters in town: Data/lumora_outskirts_spawns.json "no_monster_zones" (inside Lumora's wall and the gate front) — monsters never spawn there (a spawn circle reaching over the wall re-rolls) and never wander in on their own. Chasing a player in, and gate raids, still can.
- CRAFTING STATIONS ARE IN THE ZONE SCENE: a CraftingStations node with the 10 stations (crafting_station.gd, now @tool: set Station Id / Display Name in the Inspector; the model shows in the editor) — drag them wherever you like. crafting_world_spawner.gd only places stations from Data/crafting_placements.json for a zone whose scene has none.
- LOOT ALL: G loots every corpse within 10 m at once (the same as each loot window's Loot All; coins and loot preferences apply, anything that doesn't fit stays on the corpse). In the Esc help.
- Guards'/Oni's "made no progress toward patrol waypoint" is no longer a warning with a stack trace — a one-line note, once per waypoint (it's harmless: they carry on to the next point). A 4-minute headless run of Oni's patrol never stalled; on the server it's likely a pause (chasing a rat) outlasting the 20 s timer.

### Cat meows and the server's connections log (2026-09-23)
- Kenji and Oni meow when hailed or petted (world/cat_meow_1/2.mp3, one picked at random with a little pitch variation). tools/balance_sounds.py now also stores a volume per file ("file_db") for sounds with several variants, so each plays at the same level (the two meows were 9 dB apart); sfx.gd uses it.
- BACKPACK (test 26): its contents touched the frame (no side margin) — now an 8 px margin all round, and the column count allows for it; stack counts showed twice (the backpack added its own label on top of the slot's own count) — removed.
- CONNECTIONS LOG (dedicated server): user://logs/connections.log — one tab-separated line per CONNECT (with the client's version), LOGIN (and LOGIN FAILED with the reason), DISCONNECT, OLD VERSION (turned away for a different build), REFUSED (banned address), KICKED, BAN and UNBAN: date and time, event, IP, character, details. Never rotated or trimmed — godot.log only keeps the last five server starts. On the server: ~/.local/share/godot/app_userdata/Aldenexia-Lightfall/logs/connections.log.

### Accounts: one login, up to 8 characters (2026-09-23)
- JOIN A SERVER now logs into an ACCOUNT (account name + password; Create Account types the password twice), then shows the account's characters in a table: portrait (from character creation), name, level, race and class, zone, and "in the world" if it's online. Buttons: Enter World (or double-click a row), + Create New Character (up to 8; an empty account gets a big "Create Your First Character" button), Delete Character (type the name to confirm), Add Existing Character (claims a pre-account character with its old name + password), Log Out. The login is remembered until Log Out or quitting, so returning from character creation, a failed join or a camp-out goes straight back to the list.
- CONCURRENT LOGINS: an account can be logged in on several computers at once. The limit is still one connection per character; entering the world as a character that is already in it takes that character over, as before.
- SERVER: Scripts/account_relay.gd (class AccountRelay), created at runtime as Net's child "Accounts" so Net's RPC list is unchanged (ships as a patch). Accounts are user://server_accounts/<server>_<account>.json (salt, hash, character list). Entering the world still uses Net._rpc_login, with the account credentials packed into its password field; account characters get no password of their own. A character that belongs to an account can't be logged in or deleted with its old per-character password. Wrong account passwords lock the account for a minute after 5 tries, like characters did. connections.log gains ACCOUNT CREATED, ACCOUNT CLAIM and DELETE lines, and LOGIN lines name the account.
- MIGRATION: on start the server reads user://server_accounts/import.json once, then renames it import_done_<time>.json with the passwords removed. Each entry gives an account, its password ("password", or "password_of": a character whose existing password it copies) and its characters. Already placed on the test server: tenchijin (password lightfall) with Annadaeus, Maedianie, Shonuff and Zozuur; epheo, evangeline and wanderer each become an account of the same name, holding that character and keeping its current password. The server log lists what it did.
- Saves now record "last_zone" (the zone's display name) for the character list; older saves show Lumora Outskirts.
- TESTER_GUIDE.html: the "Getting in" steps describe accounts.
- MENU CONNECTIONS GET NO WORLD DATA: a dedicated server only replicates monsters, players, pets and NPCs to peers that have logged a character in (a visibility filter on every MultiplayerSynchronizer, net.gd _peer_in_world). Server-list probes and account requests used to receive every monster spawn and sync, printing dozens of "Node not found" / "not found in cache" errors on the player's console (63 per probe; now 0). Players still get the whole world from the moment they log in (checked: all 63 monsters, the guards and the other player).

### Every class's abilities reviewed, levels 1-8 in detail (2026-09-23)
- ENGINE (any spell can use these):
  - Passives ("passive": true) can't be cast ("...is passive — it's always working"). New working passives: Weapon Mastery (+5% melee damage), Enhanced Parry (+10% crit for 5 s after a parry), Improved Riposte (a riposte heals 3% max health), Enhanced Riposte (ripostes +10 damage; was a 30 s cast), Improved Stealth (+10% run speed while hidden), Enhanced Poison Making (poison DoTs last 25% longer, +5%). Improved Block/Parry marked passive. Enhanced Block and Improved Pickpocket are passive placeholders (see questions).
  - A spell with 0 damage no longer hits: spell power used to make every slow, curse, stun and fear hit for about 15. Such spells add a little threat instead.
  - Group spells with an "aoe_radius" reach every ally that close (they only ever healed one ally — Aetherfist's Meditation and Mend Wounds included, now 10m).
  - Area spells use their radius (point-blank was always 8 m, cones 4 m). An enemy spell with a radius splashes every enemy that close to its target (Fireball, Hurricane, Plague Cloud...).
  - Magic cones (Gravechill, Sonic Blast...) are spell damage now; they used to be computed as physical blows (strength, armour, never halved).
  - "drain_pct" heals the caster for a share of the damage (Life Drain 50%, Death Coil 20%, Harmony Blade 5%) — they used to heal the ENEMY. "self_buff" (a landed hit buffs you: Defensive Strike), "effect_chance" ("10% chance to blind" — they always applied it), "bonus_vs" / "affects_only" by monster category (Holy Strike and Smite Evil vs undead; Turn Undead only frightens undead), "ally_heal" on a burst (Radiant Burst).
  - Teleports work: Blink 10 m, Chaos Rift 8 m in a random direction (10% chance of +15% speed). They did nothing.
- DATA, levels 1-8 of all 13 other classes (about 130 spells): melee strikes 3 m reach (they reached 15 m); self-buffs that were aimed at enemies (and just hit them for their duration number) are real buffs (Tactical Stance, Battle Focus, Sacred Oath, Evasion, forms, Soul Shard, Vanish, Camouflage...); debuffs that only did the generic 5% now do what they say (Sunder Armor, Hunter's Mark — it BUFFED the enemy — Slow, Disorient, Deadly Poison, Curse of Frailty); heals over time heal at the stated rate (they healed a fifth of it); DoTs have a separate hit and lingering damage (they repeated the hit); stuns/roots/sleeps have their stated durations; Hex, Polymorph, Pacify are sleeps; Healing Wave healed enemies (chain) — now an ally heal; shields in the right places (Ancestral Shield, Mana Shield). Opening damage lowered where one ability killed a level-1 monster: Power Strike 70->35, Aimed Shot 80->40, Backstab 100->60, Ambush 120->70; weak level-1 nukes raised: Shadow Bolt 10->45, Chaos Bolt 20->45.
- DATA, levels 9-20: 32 DoTs fixed the same way, area ones given their radius; group auras that did nothing now buff (Commander's Aura, Warlord's Presence, Aura of Divinity, Temporal Mastery, Feral Roar, Song of Defiance); Withering Strike no longer heals the enemy; Dark Sovereign, Dark Reprisal, Terror Aura, War Cry, Necrotic Aura, Curse of Elements...
- Verified headless: each changed mechanic on live monsters (no hit from a slow, drains, cone/splash hitting three monsters, group heals, self-buff on hit, Blink/Chaos Rift distances, passive refusal, Smite's blind about 10% of casts) and a sweep casting all 544 non-summon spells with no script errors.

### Instant melee, upgrade spells, pickpocketing, every summon built (2026-09-23)
- MELEE ABILITIES ARE INSTANT (51): every strike/melee skill (enemy or cone within 5 m, physical point-blank, taunts) — Power Strike, Backstab, Holy Strike, the Aetherfist's strikes...
- UPGRADES: 73 Improved/Greater/Master spells are now upgrades ("upgrades": [base] in player_spells.json): each is a full copy of its base plus the described improvement (Improved Flurry 4 hits, Improved Shadow Bolt 100, Greater Mass Cure 200 in 15m...). Learning one (Player3D.learn_spell, used by scrolls) REPLACES the base in the spell book and on every action bar slot, and needs the upgrade's level (it refuses "You must be level 9 to learn Improved Flurry (it replaces Flurry of Blows)"); learning a base you've outgrown says you know the stronger one. Icons: the base's icon tinted gold (improved/greater/enhanced) or violet (master) — SpellInfo.icon_texture. An upgrade counts as its base everywhere (SpellInfo.counts_as/root_name): passives (Master Block keeps Improved Block's bonus and adds its own), spell-specific code (Improved Plague Strike, Improved Soul Leech, Master Disarm, Improved Shadow Aura), stance boosts. Master passives work: Master Block (+5% block, +5% stagger), Master Parry (strikes back 10%), Master Riposte (+15), Master Weapon Mastery (+15% melee, +5% crit), Master Stealth (first attack from hiding doesn't reveal you). 74 new scrolls (10 copper x level) — the dark classes' at Xalvyr, the rest at Ilsabet. Also fixed the bases they copy: Assassinate and Soul Reaper were heals, Volley did 5 damage, Shadow Dance hit enemies, Holy Word/Totem of Harmony/Regrowth/Anthem of Glory.
- ATTACKING REVEALS YOU from every hiding effect (Vanish, Shadow Cloak, Camouflage, Invisibility); only Invisibility used to end.
- PICKPOCKET (new, innate for Shadowblade, Troubadour, Woodstalker — existing characters get it on login): instant, 3 m, 10 s recast. 40% chance, 70% while hidden, always from behind (even mid-fight). Steals one thing the target would drop (a roll of its own loot table) or, from a humanoid/undead with an empty roll, a few coins x its level. A fail reveals you and turns the target on you. Once per enemy per player. Improved Pickpocket (7): 50%/80%, +10% coin. Master Pickpocket (14): 60%/90%, +10% coin, 10% chance to steal twice.
- SUMMONS (18 built, Scripts/summoned_pet.gd + Scenes/summoned_pet.tscn): a PetMinion wearing a borrowed monster model, tinted and scaled — TEMPORARY until real art. Spell data: "pet_kind", "pet_hp_pct", "pet_duration" (0 = until destroyed), "pet_damage_mult", "pet_crit_bonus". Voidknight Summon Wraith/Shade, Master/Eternal Wraith; Gravecaller Summon Ghoul, Abomination (+Improved, Eternal), Wraith Call; Lightmender Spiritual Weapon; Woodstalker Call Companion (wolf), Hawk/Bear/Panther Companion, Wild Call, Wild Legion; Wildspeaker Call of the Wild, Verdant Call. Timed ones fade and aren't restored at login; lasting ones are (with their spawn data). Beast Bond / Master Beast Bond now buff your companion (+10% / +25% damage dealt, less taken).
  MISSING MODELS (stand-in used): wolf (desert scavenger x1.5), hawk (bat), bear (desert scavenger x2.2), panther (desert scavenger x1.7, dark), ghoul (mummy, green), abomination (bandit x1.35, green), wraith (ghost, violet), shade (ghost, dark), spiritual weapon (ghost, gold). The animal stand-ins are unrigged critters (no animations). Verdant Call, Wild Call and Wild Legion describe 2-3 animals; one is summoned (one pet at a time).
- TESTER_GUIDE.html: a collapsible "Every control and chat command" list generated from the in-game Controls & Commands tables, and G (loot all) in the key highlights. Change-log reference: G and the GM commands.
- Verified headless: upgrade refused below its level then replaces the base (book + action bar), tinted icon, Master Block counts as Improved Block (block 30%), pickpocket from behind always works (items and coins), 73/200 from the front (40%), instant Backstab, Vanish ends on attack, wolf (timed, expires, not saved) / ghoul / wraith (saved) / hawk spawn with their models, Beast Bond on the pet; all 543 non-summon spells cast with no script errors.

### Summons permanent, and the "needs a mechanic" spells (2026-09-23)
- ALL SUMMONS ARE PERMANENT (until destroyed): the 13 that had a timer from their descriptions (Spiritual Weapon 15 s, companions/ghoul/abominations/Wraith Call 20-50 s) no longer fade, and come back at login like the others.
- COMMANDING PRESENCE (Blademaster 6): a 15-minute group buff — allies in 10m draw 10% less threat, the Blademaster 15% more ("self_modifiers": the caster's own modifiers on a group spell).
- RALLYING CRY (Blademaster 4): heals you and allies in 10m for 5% health and gives you +10 armor for 15 s ("caster_buff"; new "armor_bonus" modifier adds to armor class).
- SIPHON ESSENCE (was Siphon Mana, Gravecaller 4; internal name unchanged so saves keep it): drains 30 mana from a player or a spell-casting monster ("casts_spells"); anything else — every monster today — loses life instead (a 30 spell hit) and all of it heals you.
- DEATH PACT (Gravecaller 8): sacrifice your pet (alive, within 15m) to restore 35% of your health; 60 s recast.
- GUARDIAN SPIRIT (Lightmender 8): on an ally for 15 s, the first killing blow leaves them at 20% health instead ("cheat_death" modifier, checked on every change to current_hp so all damage paths are covered; works on a remote group member's own machine).
- HEALING RECEIVED: new "healing_received" modifier scales every heal and heal-over-time tick. BEACON OF HOPE (Lightsworn 8): you and allies in 10m receive 25% more healing for 15 s.
- ENHANCED BLOCK (Lightsworn 5 passive): +10% block chance and 10% less magic damage (ready for when monsters cast spells).
- Verified headless: threat_mult +0.15 on the caster, Rallying Cry 5% heal and armor 29 -> 39, Siphon Essence 18 damage / 18 healed on a monster, ghoul permanent, Death Pact sacrifice + 35% heal, Guardian Spirit catches a lethal hit at exactly 20% then is gone, Beacon heal 100 -> 125, Enhanced Block chance.

### Knockback, moving breaks casting, outstanding_items.txt (2026-09-23)
- KNOCKBACK: a spell's "knockback" (metres) pushes a monster straight away from the caster over 0.25 s — walls stop it, and it does nothing else meanwhile (monster3d.gd knockback(); a client's puppet asks the server with apply_networked_knockback). Power Strike, Earth Strike (its original knockback) and Titan's Strike (now also hits enemies within 8 m of the target) push 4 m. monsters.json "knockback_immune": true opts a monster out (none are yet). Measured: pushed 4.3 m single-player, 3.4-4.1 m as seen by a client on a server.
- MOVING BREAKS A CAST: a player who drifts more than 0.25 m from where a cast began (walking, knocked back, falling) loses it — "You moved, and your spell fizzles." Turning in place and jumping straight up don't. Troubadours are exempt (songs are sung on the move). A knockback also cancels a monster's cast (monsters don't cast yet; this is the rule for when they do). Verified: a Gravecaller's Shadow Bolt breaks when they step 1 m, holds when still; a Troubadour's song keeps going.
- NEW outstanding_items.txt (project root): every unfinished item, open question, stand-in model and unverified behaviour in one place.

### Languages, the Vol'kyne (2026-09-23)
- LANGUAGES (Scripts/languages.gd, Data/languages.json): 15 languages — Common, Aevaryn, Khuzdul, Kithren, Cant of the Wanderers, Twilari, Grommak, Grommish, Kry'thuun, Orkish, Sithrak, Djhanid, Old Thallian, High Arcanum, Goblish. Skills 0-100 in Global.player_data["languages"], not level-capped.
  - Starting skills by race: humans Common 100; half-elves Twilari 100, Common 75, Aevaryn 40; halflings Cant 100, Common 75; elves, dwarves, gnomes, Vol'kyne their own 100 + Common 50; half-orcs Orkish 100 + Common 40; trolls, ogres, lizardkin their own 100 + Common 15. Existing characters get theirs at login.
  - Understanding: your skill is roughly the share of words you get, short everyday words first; the rest is scrambled — the same word always the same way, in the language's own sound (each has a syllable palette). Dialects understand each other partly (Grommak/Grommish 50%, Aevaryn/Twilari 50%, Common/Cant 50%, Twilari->Common 30%, Orkish/Goblish 30%).
  - Speaking: a language dropdown next to the chat channel (your languages with skill), or /language [name]. Default: Common if you have 50+, else your best. Low skill mispronounces: words beyond your skill + 25 come out garbled even to fluent listeners; your own screen shows what you meant. Lines show the language: "Dessik says, in Khuzdul, '...'". Say, shout, party and tell all carry it (inside the message text: Net's chat RPCs are unchanged).
  - Learning: a scroll of a language's basics starts it at 1 (Ilsabet: Aevaryn, Khuzdul, Kithren, Cant, Twilari; Xalvyr: Kry'thuun, Grommish, Grommak, Orkish, Sithrak, Goblish; Sahren: Djhanid; Old Thallian: a 2% drop from undead; High Arcanum: item exists, not sold yet). Each line heard in a language you're learning — players, NPCs (hailing guards counts), monsters — may raise it: 8% x (1 - skill/100) x (1 + (INT+WIS-20)/40), at most one roll per language every 4 s. Speaking it counts too when someone who knows it hears you (a player whose race speaks it, or an NPC who does). About 60-70 lines for the first few points; slower as you improve.
  - NPCs and monsters: NPCs have a "language" (Inspector field on guards and vendors; all Common today) and their lines scramble for you ({keyword} links stay readable). Monsters: "language" in monsters.json, else by category (humanoids Common, undead Old Thallian); goblins speak Goblish. Monster death lines and gate-raid shouts use it (raid shouts are scrambled on each player's own machine).
- NPCs UNDERSTAND ONLY THEIR LANGUAGES: a keyword said (or clicked) in a language the NPC doesn't speak gets "Private Corwin doesn't understand you. (You're speaking Grommish.)". NPCs can speak several ("extra_languages", Inspector field on guards and vendors) and answer in the one you addressed them in (Languages.npc_speaks / voice_of). Sahren speaks Common and Djhanid. Dustwalkers stay Common (they and the Djhanid hate each other).
- EPHEO fixed on the server (Vol'kyne stats bug): DEX 12, INT 16 (his 4 creation points on the real racial 12), CHA 11, resistances magic/psychic +10, fire -5. Original kept as test_epheo_character_stats.before_volkyne_fix.json.
- VOL'KYNE: the Dark Elf race is now shown as Vol'kyne everywhere (character creation lists "Vol'kyne (Dark Elf)", sorted by name; character sheet, account list, class restrictions, deity/faction/scribe text). The internal key stays dark_elf, so existing characters and models are unchanged. Global.race_display_name().
- FIX: character creation looked up racial base stats by the lower-cased display name, which never matched "dark-elf" — Dark Elves (now Vol'kyne) were created with flat 10s. Now by race key. Existing ones (Epheo) keep their stats.
- /language added to the in-game Controls & Commands list, the tester guide and this file's reference.
- Verified headless: a troll starts Grommish 100 / Common 15 and speaks Grommish; hearing Common/Grommak (dialect)/Khuzdul scrambles consistently; mispronunciation; learning 1 -> 5 in 64 heard lines; a guard's line scrambled for a troll; a goblin raid shout in Goblish; Vol'kyne base stats; on a real server a human hears a troll's Grommish scrambled while another troll understands it.

### Test 27: doubled windows, a new character inheriting the last one's items (2026-09-23)
- DOUBLED UI / old windows on the character creation screen: leaving the world through a lost connection (the update's server restart), a failed join or the character list went back to the menu WITHOUT removing the game windows — only camping out did. The next Enter World built a second HUD on top, and the stale windows (showing the old character) stayed on screen, even on character creation. Now Global.free_game_ui() removes every leftover game window (HUD, chat pop-outs, popups: any root CanvasLayer/Window that isn't the current scene or an autoload) whenever the main menu or character creation opens, and _spawn_hud() removes any old HUD before building one.
- NEW CHARACTER GOT THE LAST CHARACTER'S ITEMS: character creation's starting inventory reset the bag slots, equipment and the first bag only — the contents of every other bag and the whole bank survived from the character played before in the same session, and the orphan-rescue on load moved them into the new character. Inventory.reset_for_new_character() now clears all of it: when a character is cleared (joining a server, disconnecting), when a starting inventory is built, and when a character with no saved inventory loads. The account screen (play one character, then make another without restarting) made this easy to hit; before accounts it needed the same session too.
- Server data: Grep reset to his starter kit (he had copies of Zozuur's loose items, three bags' contents and his bank; Zozuur still has his own). Original kept as test_grep_character_stats.before_inventory_fix.json.
- Verified on a local server: play character A (items in a second bag and the bank), drop the connection, back at the menu (0 game windows), create character B in the same session: one HUD (12 nodes, no duplicates) and only B's starter kit.

### Starving / Thirsty, debuff borders, Weak Poison (2026-09-23)
- STARVING and THIRSTY show on the status (buff) window when food or drink runs out (satiety / thirst at 0): the bread and water-flask icons washed red with a yellow border, marked as debuffs, with what they do (no health/stamina, or no mana, regeneration until you eat/drink). They are the player's state, not saved effects: they vanish the moment you eat or drink, and can't be right-click cancelled.
- EVERY DEBUFF on the status window now has a yellow border round its icon (buffs keep theirs plain).
- WEAK POISON: snakes, spiderlings, spiders and blighted spiders have a 25% chance on a landed hit to poison you — 5 damage every 6 seconds for a minute (a new bite refreshes it, doesn't stack). "A Sand Viper's bite poisons you!" Green skull on the status window; cure spells remove it. Data: monsters.json "on_hit_effect" {name, chance, tick_damage, tick_interval, duration, message} — any monster can have one.
- DISEASE: the undead (skeletons, mummies, ghosts, mirage phantoms, Sergeant Halvek) have a 25% chance on a landed hit to infect you — 5 damage every 6 seconds for 90 seconds, refreshed (not stacked) by another hit. "A Fallen Scout Skeleton's rotting touch sickens you!" Necromancy icon with the debuff border; cure spells remove it. Poison and Disease can be on you at the same time (one of each).

### Tank stances: Blademaster, Lightsworn, Voidknight group bonuses and auras (2026-09-23)
- BLADEMASTER stances (all at level 1): Hold the Line (defensive: -10% damage, +15 block, +25% threat; group takes 10% less damage), Warpath (offensive: +15% damage, +10% attack speed, -10 block, -35% threat; group +10% attack speed), Veteran's Stance (balanced: +7% damage, +7 block, +5% crit; group +10 hit chance).
- LIGHTSWORN stances (all at level 1): Aegis of Dawn (defensive: -10% damage, +15 block, +25% threat, +15% healing received; you and the group: 10% chance on a melee hit to be healed for 5% of max health), Dawn's Wrath (offensive: +15% damage, Holy Strike and Smite Evil 25% stronger, -10 block, -35% threat; group +10% damage), Oathkeeper (balanced: +7% damage, +7 block, +2 health regen; group +10% healing received).
- VOIDKNIGHT stances keep their stats and gain group bonuses: Necrotic Bastion (you and the group: 10% chance on a melee hit to lifetap — 8 + 2/level spirit damage, half back as health), Shadow Ascendant (group +10% crit), Dusk Walker (group +10% movement speed). Level 1 set explicitly.
- Group bonuses reach group members within 10 m, like the Aetherfist's; the same stance from two players doesn't stack.
- AURAS, a different look per tank (class_stances.json "aura_style"): Voidknight "drain" — streaks pulled in from ~1.8 m, faint far out and brightening as they reach the knight, with cold wisps sinking at the feet (the drain, shown); Lightsworn "radiant" — a warm glow, bright motes rising and soft shafts of light; Blademaster "sparks" — bursts of spark streaks flicking out and falling. Colours per stance (e.g. Necrotic Bastion sickly green, Aegis of Dawn gold, Warpath blood red, Hold the Line steel). The Aetherfist keeps its rising motes. Everyone sees them (current_stance is replicated).
- STANCE ICONS for all nine (Assets/icons/spells/stance_*.png, recoloured from existing art in each stance's colour); the Voidknight's used to be the sword/shield item icons.
- Verified: each class's stance bar at level 1 with the right modifiers/group bonuses; Aegis heal and Necrotic lifetap procs (~10%); auras rendered in a real window and compared (screenshots).

### Test 29: the rats that never fought back (found), creature attack words, copyable chat (2026-09-24)
- "RATS NEVER FIGHT BACK" FOUND, watching the live server with Zozuur standing by two of them: the rat was in CHASE, targeting him, 2.09 m away with a 2.0 m reach — and not moving at all (another frozen 19 m away). A chasing monster only ever moved along its navigation path; when navigation said it had arrived (at a stale spot, or on ground where the navmesh gives no path) while the target was still out of reach, it stood still forever and never swung (it attacked only if you stepped onto it). Now a chasing monster with nothing to follow walks straight at its target (walls still block it), and re-aims its navigation whenever it "arrived" but its target is out of reach. Verified at the same spot on a local server: the rat follows when you step back and attacks. This is why it could never be reproduced elsewhere — it depends on the ground.
- DIAGNOSTIC (server log, "[monster-diag]"): a monster hurt 6 s ago that never swung logs one line with its state, target, distances, reach, attack timer, navigation state, position and spawn point. To remove once the rats are confirmed fixed.
- SPAWN SYNC FIX: every monster that appeared on a client logged "Node 'CombatNode:current_hp' not found" and its starting health didn't arrive (the client applied the server's spawn state before the monster had built its CombatNode). The CombatNode now exists from construction. 0 errors for 66 monsters (was 62 of 63).
- CREATURES DON'T USE WEAPONS in the log: animals bite, claw or scratch; reptiles bite; insects bite or pinch; monsters.json "attack_verbs" overrides (sand vipers and spiders bite, sand stingers sting or pinch, bats bite or scratch). "A sand viper bites you for 5 damage!" — also in what nearby players read. Humanoids and undead keep weapon words.
- BLOOD RITUAL lasts 30 seconds (was 8).
- PETS COME BACK WHEN YOU RESPAWN: waking at your bind point brings your pet to your side at once (it used to walk the whole way), stops its fight, and every monster within 40 m of where it was forgets it (a new server request, Monster.forget_attacker) — a monster with nobody left on its threat list goes home instead of chasing the pet to town. Verified: a ghoul fighting 800 m away is beside you after respawning; the monster drops it and goes idle.
- APPRAISAL TRAINS PERCEPTION: every appraisal (I) raises the Perception skill, and each 10 points add +1 to the appraisal roll; the abilities book shows it. Perception will matter more later (hidden enemies, traps, secrets).
- CHAT TEXT IS SELECTABLE: drag to select in any chat tab (General, Combat, your own tabs), right-click for Copy / Select All. The log still never takes keyboard focus, so movement keys keep working.
- Live-server housekeeping: an observer character (zzobserver) was created to watch the rats and then deleted (kept in deleted/ as usual).

### Every item in items.json, "known" on every kind of scroll, Tin Shield (2026-09-24)
- ONE ITEM FILE: the 188 crafting items (materials, crafted gear/food/potions, kits, tools, ammo, 65 "Recipe:" scrolls) moved from the generated Data/crafting_items.json into Data/items.json, which now holds all 612 items in the game (spell and language scrolls were already there) and is the one place to edit any item's properties. crafting_items.json is deleted; inventory_autoload.gd no longer merges it (an old base build's copy is simply ignored).
- tools/export_crafting.py no longer writes item definitions over anything: it APPENDS only items that don't exist yet in items.json (a new recipe's output and its scroll, a new material) and never changes an existing entry. Changing an existing item's stats in Crafting.xlsx therefore no longer reaches the game — edit items.json. Recipes and gathering nodes still come from the workbook as before.
- ALREADY KNOWN on recipe and language scrolls: the vendor's "✔ known" tag (greyed name), the green tick badge on an inventory slot and the inspect popup now cover recipe scrolls (the recipe is in known_recipes) and language primers (the language is at 1 point or more), not only spell scrolls. Aldric showed Zozuur's Tin Shortsword recipe as unknown because only spells were checked. ItemInspector.teaches_known_spell() became teaches_known() / known_kind() ("spell", "recipe", "language"); the shop tooltip says which.
- TIN SHIELD (new, Blacksmithing): Crafting.xlsx Blacksmithing row smith_tin_shield — 2 Tin Ingot + 1 Fir Planks at a forge, skill 3 (trivial 18), 90% / 8% crit, 7 s. Offhand shield, AC 3, +1 Stamina, value 10 cp. "Recipe: Tin Shield" scroll, 30 copper, sold by Aldric after the Tin Shortsword recipe. Icon: the shield art tinted tin (make_icon_variants.py). Workbook backup: Crafting.before_tin_shield.xlsx.
- Verified headless: all 612 items load; every vendor stock id, recipe ingredient/output/scroll resolves; with a stand-in player, the shop row for a known recipe shows "✔ known" (tooltip "You already know this recipe."), an unknown one doesn't; language primer and spell scroll report known; the inspect popup says "✔ You already know this recipe."; the tin shield icon loads.

### Server trust, telemetry, regression suite (2026-09-24)
- SERVER TRUST (new Scripts/server_trust.gd, called from net.gd _rpc_save_character()): every uploaded save is checked
  against the one the server holds. Name/class/race/sex/base stats/birthday can't change; XP may rise only by the kill XP
  the SERVER awarded since the last save (new ledger: monster3d.gd die() -> Net.note_kill()) plus the XP of quests the
  save newly completes (repeatables and Kenji included) — the 50-XP rounding margin only applies to XP really earned;
  the level must match the XP; coin may rise only from a per-character allowance (1,000 copper at login, +200 a minute,
  at most an hour's worth — so saving often gains nothing) plus quest coin; skills at most 4 x level (or what you had);
  spells must exist and be your class's; recipes must exist; languages at most one point per 4 s (and at least 1 s
  between saves for a point); surname at level 10. Anything impossible keeps the stored value and is logged ("TRUST"
  lines in the server log). Game masters are trusted. Items are logged, not refused (yet). The old surname-only check is
  folded in.
- TELEMETRY (dedicated server): user://logs/telemetry.jsonl — login, logout, every kill (character, class, level,
  monster, XP), every save's changes (XP, level, coin, deaths and what killed you — players now count "deaths" and
  "last_death_by" — items gained/lost, quests completed), and every trust anomaly. New tools/telemetry_report.py
  (fetches the log over ssh by default): per class hours / XP / kills / deaths / coin per hour, time to each level,
  most-killed monsters, what kills players, quests, items, anomalies.
- REGRESSION SUITE (new tools/tests/, tools/run_tests.sh): 9 test files, ~1,440 checks, headless, about a minute:
  data integrity (every shop, recipe, quest and scroll id resolves; 9 pieces per armour set), server trust, quests
  (multi-item, repeatables, the foundation stone, Kenji, Reyna's hail), crafting (Tin Shield picker, How many, split,
  Deconstruct, bags in bags), armour types / shields / set bonus, racial traits, travel / evac / gates / binding, names and
  surnames, and combat (poison vs combat music, language rate, /stuck). tools/update.sh runs it before any export and
  stops on a failure (--skip-tests to skip).
- Verified end to end: a bot client joined a local dedicated server, created a level-1 character and uploaded a save
  claiming Arcanist, level 15, 999,999 XP and 99 platinum: the server kept Blademaster, level 1, 0 XP and the old coin,
  and logged four anomalies; the report read the log. (That run found the XP-margin and per-save allowances loophole,
  fixed above.) Net's RPC list unchanged.


### Macros, account-shared macros (2026-09-24)
- MACROS (new Scripts/macros.gd, Scripts/macro_panel.gd): EverQuest-style. Abilities book (K) has a new Macros tab
  (/macro opens it): 24 macros for this character, and a "Shared (account)" page of 24 every character on the account
  sees. A macro = name, icon (any spell you know; "Automatic" uses the first /cast line's spell), up to 5 lines. Click
  a button to edit, Save / Clear / Try it; drag it to the action bar (purple border, "M" badge, shows its spell's recast;
  hotkeys 1-= run it). Lines run through the chat box, so any chat command works; new /cast <spell> (also in chat),
  /target <name>, /pause <seconds>. %t / %T / %s. A /cast that fails stops the macro (the group isn't told about a
  spell that never went off); pressing a running macro does nothing.
- Aliases: /g and /gsay = /party (before, /g ran /gm!), /pa = /party, /ca = /camp (kept when /pause and /cast arrived).
- SHARED MACROS ON THE SERVER: stored in the ACCOUNT file (server_accounts/<server>_<account>.json "shared_macros"). At
  login net.gd appends the account's list to the character text sent to the client (text append, no new RPC, no
  re-stringify); a save whose list differs from what THAT character last had is filed into the account (so a second
  character online with an older copy can't overwrite it). A character with no account keeps them in its own save;
  single-player/LAN keeps them in user://shared_macros.json. Lists are cleaned on the server (24 x 5 lines x 120 chars).
- Net RPC list unchanged (no new base needed). Tests: tools/tests/test_macros.gd (46 checks); suite 1,481 checks pass.
  Verified end to end with a local server and a bot: account created, character created on it, shared macro saved ->
  in the account file -> back on the next login; the character macro stays with the character.

### Travel sites: Spires, Ruins, Rifts (2026-09-24)
- The three Outskirts ley-stones are REMOVED (Data/ley_lines.json "nodes": []). Per the map (Aldenexia Map.png) each
  travel class has its OWN sites in different zones: Arcanist Spires (raised by the Lycaeum before the Lightfall),
  Wildspeaker Ruins (older than any city; the Green remembers), Chaosborn Rifts (wounds the Lightfall tore). The first
  will be the Dustwind Spire, hidden off the beaten path in its own area.
- ley_line_node.gd: a node's "class" makes it that class's only (no class = all three, used by tests). The owner sees its
  name and attunes; the other two travel classes see "Arcanist Spire" etc. and get a line saying it isn't for them;
  everyone else sees "Old Tower" / "Overgrown Ruins" / "Scarred Ground". Stand-in shapes: a tall slim blue spire, a
  green centre stone in a broken ring, a jagged leaning violet shard. player_travel.gd offers only your class's
  attuned sites (an old attunement to another class's site is ignored); "You aren't attuned to any of the Arcanist
  Spires yet".
- Tests: test_travel.gd (class-only sites, chooser), test_data.gd (Outskirts has none). Manual's travel section rewritten.

### Zoning: Outskirts <-> Dustwind Plateaus; Dustwind's monsters; sheets and JSON kept in step (2026-09-24)
- ZONING (dedicated servers; single-player just changes scene; LAN games say it isn't supported). Data/zones.json lists every
  zone: scene + port_offset (Outskirts 0 = the login server, Dustwind +10, Ashfall +11: live 8910 / 8920 / 8921). Each zone
  runs as its own server process sharing the character and account files: tools/run_world.sh starts them all (and stops
  them all gracefully); a server started with --zone=<id> [--base-port=] runs that zone. The login server owns the world
  clock and saves it every 20 s; the other zones follow it (catching up by the time since it was written).
- Scripts/zone_line.gd (ZoneLine, @tool Area3D in a zone's ZoneLines folder): Target Zone, Target Marker (a Marker3D under
  the other zone's Markers), Size; an orange see-through wall with its destination in the editor, invisible in game. Walking
  in: Net.zone_travel() saves the character with "zone" + "zone_in", waits for the server to confirm the save, leaves, and
  joins that zone's server (same address, its port), arriving at the marker. Placed: Outskirts "To Dustwind Plateaus" in
  the middle of the south edge (-1500, 0) + arrival marker "dustwind_plateaus_zone" (-1460, 0); Dustwind "To Lumora
  Outskirts" on the north edge behind the user's "lumora_outskirts_zone" marker.
- Login: a character logs in where it is ("zone" in the save; older characters are in the Outskirts). Any other zone's
  server answers "zone_redirect" (through the existing login-failed message: no new RPC, no new base) and the client joins
  the right one; a per-server zone hint (user://zone_hints.json) makes the next login go straight there. Account lists are
  re-read at every login (another zone's server may have added a character).
- Server trust: a save that changes zone is kept only if the player is at a zone line to that zone (ZoneLine.covers(),
  12 m slack), is going home to its bind zone ("@bind": death / gate), or is a GM — else the stored zone is kept and a
  TRUST line logged. Accepted moves drop last_position (you arrive at the marker).
- Bind points now remember their zone (bind_zone). Dying or casting a gate while bound in another zone takes you there.
- The character list shows the zone the character is in: saves name their "zone" (a zone trip's last save already names
  the destination), the server sets it when it accepts a zone change, and the account listing reads it. Verified live:
  after zoning, the list reads "Zzwalker in Dustwind Plateaus".
- Verified end to end with a local world (run_world.sh --base-port=8990) and a bot: Outskirts -> Dustwind arriving at
  (0, 0, -474); a faked zone change refused; a fresh login on the login port sent to Dustwind; Dustwind -> Outskirts
  arriving at (-1460, 0, 0); all servers stop gracefully.
- DUSTWIND MONSTERS from the Zone Spawn Sheet (DustwindPlateaus tab): 13 new in monsters.json (plateau_spider,
  dustwalker_ambusher, gravedigger_skeleton, sunmaddened_berserker, djhanid_nomad, restless_corpse, djhanid_sand_walker,
  sand_stinger_elite, spider_broodmother, wraith, risen_priest, tomb_guardian, grave_abomination) and 6 brought in line
  (wild_dog = Feral Desert Hound, sand_stinger = Scorched Sandstinger, sunmaddened_wanderer 4-7, dustwalker_scout,
  dustwalker_ravager, dustwalker_warlord). Levels from the sheet; stats from a level curve (elites x1.8 health, bosses
  x2.6); looks borrowed (model_from + tint/scale); special attacks as on-hit ailments. The Djhanid are passive with aggro
  range 0 (they only fight back). Casting and summoning (Risen Priest, Broodmother) are noted, not built.
- On-hit ailments can carry modifiers now; new ailments (status window text + icon, cures remove them): strong_poison,
  weakening_venom, sundered_armor, crippled, blinded, dazed, withering_touch, bleeding, grave_miasma.
- monsters.json "loot_from": a monster with no loot table borrows another's (the new ones use their base type's).
- Data/dustwind_plateaus_spawns.json rewritten (the old draft had comments and no positions): 28 spawn points, first-pass
  layout; zone-in kept clear. Verified: a bot in Dustwind saw hounds, stingers, scouts and plateau spiders, levels in range.
  Old Ashfall draft parked as Data/ashfall_dunes_spawns.draft.jsonc. Dustwind navmesh rebaked with the mesa.
- monsters.json is THE source of truth for monsters (its _comment documents every field). Spawn files no longer carry
  min_level/max_level (the game never used them; the manual did — tools/make_manual.py now reads monsters.json).
- tools/check_monster_sheet.py: compares the Zone Spawn Sheet with monsters.json (ids, level ranges, a row for everything
  that spawns); --write fixes the sheet (backup kept). Ran it: column A of each tab is now "Monster ID (monsters.json)",
  Dustwind ids filled in, 8 rows added to the Outskirts tab (the named mobs, blighted spider, mirage phantom, Sunmaddened).
  tools/update.sh warns when they differ. Backup: Zone Spawn Sheet.before_sync_2026-09-24.xlsx.
- Tests: test_zones.gd (zones.json, every zone line's target zone and marker exist, covers(), the server's zone-change
  check), new test_monsters.gd (every monster: fields, level range, a look, loot_from, known ailments; built zones' spawn
  files name real monsters and set no levels; Dustwind monsters load). Suite: 2,383 checks pass.

  walkable slope 50 degrees; and a safety net — more than 0.5 m under the terrain surface (slipped through a steep slope)
