  matching the drag pattern already used everywhere else. Position
  persistence (only chat had it before) added via a new shared
  `WindowPosition` helper (`save()`/`load_position_into()` — position only,
  so it doesn't fight the loot window's own auto-resize-to-content —
  `load_full_into()` for resizable windows) to buff bar, loot window,
  character sheet, and backpack. Stance bar deliberately left non-draggable
  — it's docked to a fixed position above the action bar by design.

**Loot table data fix, at the user's direction** (`Data/solgrave_expanse_loot.json`)
- Investigated "almost no money dropping": not a code bug — `edge_spawns`
  (bandit/skeleton/goblin's actual loot table, despite the zone-name
  mismatch with where they spawn — the lookup searches all zones regardless
  of name) had skeleton and goblin already dropping coin, but bandit had
  none at all (only a 10% dagger chance and 20% leather scrap chance). Added
  `copper_coin` (60% chance, 5–20) to bandit's table per the user's request
  to have all humanoid mobs drop coin.

---

## 2026-09-13 — Session 26: Player/guard 3D models, monster chase fix, advanced loot, camera look modes

### New Features

**Player 3D model + Mixamo animations** (`player3d.tscn`, `player3d.gd`)
- Replaced the placeholder capsule (visual AND collision — radius 1.08m, i.e.
  2.16m wide, never actually tuned to human scale) with a real Mixamo
  character model. Collision resized to a sane radius 0.35m / height 1.8m,
  repositioned so its bottom sits at the node's local Y=0 (the model's own
  feet are there too), replacing the old "capsule centered on the node
  origin" convention.
- 8 Mixamo animations (idle, walk, run, jump, sit, attack_horizontal,
  attack_downward, death) imported to `models/player/*.fbx`, each stripped
  down to just its real clip (`mixamo_com` — Mixamo FBX exports also contain
  a bogus identical-length `Take 001` placeholder in every file) and merged
  into one shared `AnimationLibrary` at `models/player/player_animations.res`
  — built once via a headless script rather than kept as 8 separate imported
  scenes, so the game only loads keyframe data instead of instancing 8x the
  mesh/skeleton just to steal one animation each.
- `player3d.gd`: `_setup_animations()` loads that library onto
  `$Character/AnimationPlayer`, replacing the FBX's own default `""` library
  (which owns the raw `Take 001`/`mixamo_com` clips and silently blocks
  `add_animation_library("")` if not removed first — the one real bug hit
  while wiring this up). `_update_animation()` (called after `move_and_slide()`
  each physics tick) picks the animation by priority: mid-attack → the
  attack anim chosen for this swing → airborne → jump → sitting → sit →
  `current_speed` above/below `WALK_SPEED+0.5` → run/walk → idle.
  `_trigger_attack_animation()` (called from both `attack_current_target()`
  and `perform_melee_attack()`) randomly picks attack_horizontal or
  attack_downward per swing, held via `_current_attack_anim` for the
  duration of the `attacking` flag. `die()` plays the death animation
  directly (bypasses `_update_animation()`, which stops running once `dying`
  short-circuits `_physics_process`).
- Facing was backwards on the first pass — fixed with a 180°-Y transform on
  the `Character` node (`Transform3D(-1,0,~0, 0,1,0, ~0,0,-1, ...)`).

**Guard 3D model + animations** (`guard_npc.tscn`, `guard_npc.gd`)
- Same model, same shared `player_animations.res` (identical skeleton, no
  duplication needed) — swapped in for the guards' flat gray capsule mesh,
  same facing-fix transform. Collision left as-is (was already reasonably
  human-scaled, unlike the player's).
- `_update_animation()`: idle/walk off actual velocity magnitude (guards
  move at one fixed nav-agent speed, no run/walk distinction to key off of —
  `run` is in the shared library but unused here). `_play_attack_animation()`
  picks a random swing and holds it via `_attack_anim_timer` (counts down
  from the clip's real length) so sustained combat reads as continuous
  swinging rather than snapping to idle between 1.5s attack-cooldown hits.
  `play_death_animation()` added but not called anywhere — guards have no
  health/damage-taking system at all (monsters only ever attack the player),
  so nothing currently puts one in a state to die; one-line hookup whenever
  that gets built.
- Guards were both facing the wrong way in the outskirts entryway — rotated
  90° in `lumora_outskirts3d.tscn` (world-space Y rotation on each GuardNPC
  instance, not a model-local fix like the one above).
- `respond_to_hail()` now calls a new `_face_player()` first — looks up the
  player via the `"player"` group and `look_at()`s them (Y-locked, no
  tilting), so a hailed guard visibly turns to face you. They don't turn
  back afterward — same as most NPCs that turn to greet you and just stay
  that way.

**Buff bar** (`buff_bar.tscn`, `buff_bar.gd`, added to `player3d._spawn_hud()`)
- Draggable vertical HUD panel (same drag pattern as the loot window), one
  row per entry in `combat_node.active_effects` — reads that Dictionary
  directly rather than tracking its own list, so anything already going
  through `apply_effect()` (stances, spell buffs/debuffs, the new campfire
  buff below) shows up automatically with zero extra wiring per-effect.
  Rows only rebuild when the *set* of active effects changes; countdown
  labels update every frame without rebuilding.
- `_resolve_effect_display(effect_name)` turns an `active_effects` key into
  a name + "what it does" description by checking, in order: stance data
  (`stance_<id>` → `class_stances.json`), the player's own spell cache
  (`apply_effect`'s effect_name matches `spell_name` exactly for every
  spell-driven buff/debuff already in `player3d.gd` — e.g. `blood_ritual`,
  `necrotic_grasp`, `shadow_aura`), then a new small
  `ENVIRONMENTAL_EFFECT_DESCRIPTIONS` table for non-spell effects (currently
  just `campfire_warmth`), falling back to a titled raw name if nothing
  matches. Icon: each row reserves a blank bordered 28×28 box — no icon
  assets exist yet, `_build_row()`'s `icon_box` is where to wire real
  textures in later.

**Advanced loot preferences** (`corpse_loot_window.gd`, `monster3d.gd`,
`global.gd`)
- `Global.player_data["loot_preferences"]`: item_id → `"loot"` / `"ignore"`
  / `"sell"`, via new `Global.get_loot_preference()`/`set_loot_preference()`
  (the latter saves immediately, same as other player_data mutations).
- `corpse_loot_window.gd`: each row gets 3 mutually-exclusive checkboxes
  (`ButtonGroup`-backed) — checking one calls `set_loot_preference()` *and*
  resolves the current drop immediately (loot/sell → `_apply_drop()` +
  remove from list; ignore → just remove, nothing granted).
- `monster3d.gd`: `die()` now runs `pending_loot` through a new
  `_auto_process_loot()` before `is_lootable` is computed. Currency is
  always auto-collected (no checkbox — collecting coins was never something
  worth a manual click) and never reaches the window at all anymore. Every
  other drop is checked against the saved preference: `loot`/`sell` →
  auto-loot + a GameLog line, `ignore` → discarded silently, no preference →
  left in `pending_loot` for the window. If auto-processing empties
  `pending_loot` entirely, `is_lootable` is false and right-clicking the
  corpse never opens a window at all — matches EQ's advanced-loot autoloot
  behavior (fully-decided corpses just resolve, no popup).
- Junk was folded into Sell (confirmed with the user they're mechanically
  identical — no separate "Junk" checkbox, just Loot/Ignore/Sell).
- Known pre-existing gap, NOT fixed here: `_apply_drop()`
  (`corpse_loot_window.gd`) only ever adds 1 unit to inventory regardless of
  a drop's `quantity` — a stack of 5 skins only grants 1. `_auto_process_loot()`
  inherits the same behavior for consistency; worth fixing later.

### Bug Fixes

**Monsters never chased or fought back when attacked — the real bug**
(`monster3d.gd`)
- `add_threat()` recorded damage into `aggro_table` but never changed
  `current_state` — the ONLY thing that ever moved a monster out of
  IDLE/PATROL into CHASE was `can_see_player()`, a pure proximity check
  (capped at `MAX_AGGRO_DISTANCE`=5m, and as tight as ~1.67m for
  `behavior_type=="passive"` mobs — smaller than the default 2m
  `attack_range`, so even point-blank melee against a passive mob could
  fail to register). Landing a hit would damage the monster and add threat,
  but if that same swing didn't also happen to satisfy the separate,
  narrower sight check, the monster just stood there taking hits forever.
  Fixed by having `add_threat()` itself force `change_state(State.CHASE)`
  whenever amount > 0 and the monster is currently IDLE or PATROL — being
  attacked now always starts a fight, regardless of the vision-range math.
  (A pure miss still generates 0 threat and won't wake a monster on its
  own — you need to land at least one hit.)

**Chat window could permanently lock out player movement**
(`game_log_window.gd`)
- `chat_input` (the chat `LineEdit`) never released keyboard focus after
  being clicked — not on Enter, not on Escape. `player3d.gd` already
  (correctly) blocks movement while any `LineEdit` has focus, so one click
  into the chat box left you stuck until you clicked it again. Separately,
  `W`/`S` are bound to both `move_forward`/`move_backward` *and* Godot's
  built-in `ui_up`/`ui_down` focus-navigation (checked `project.godot`), and
  a single-line field doesn't consume vertical arrows itself, so those
  keypresses fell through to Godot's default "hand focus to some other
  control" behavior — read by the user as "pressing forward/back moves the
  selected tab." Fixed: `_on_chat_input_submitted()` now calls
  `chat_input.release_focus()` after clearing the text; a new
  `_on_chat_input_gui_input()` handler releases focus on Escape too; and
  `chat_input.focus_neighbor_top`/`focus_neighbor_bottom` are pinned to
  itself so W/S can never hand focus off while genuinely typing.
- Added the flip side while in here: `_unhandled_input()` on
  `game_log_window.gd` now grabs `chat_input` focus on Enter when nothing
  else is focused (so you don't have to click the box first) —
  `set_input_as_handled()`'d so it can't double-fire.

**Mouse cursor could get stuck hidden, or mouselook could get silently
cancelled** (`global.gd`, `camera_controller.gd`, `player3d.gd`,
`corpse_loot_window.gd`, `backpack_ui.gd`, `character_sheet.gd`)
- Mouse-mode state was scattered across 6 files, each independently
  hardcoding `MOUSE_MODE_VISIBLE`/`MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED` with no shared
  source of truth for "is mouselook actually on." Two concrete bugs:
  `corpse_loot_window.gd` forced the mouse to CAPTURED on close even if
  mouselook was never enabled (hidden mouse after looting); every other
  window (pause menu, character sheet, backpack) forced VISIBLE on close,
  silently cancelling mouselook if it happened to be on. Fixed with a new
  `Global.mouselook_enabled` bool (F12/`camera_controller.gd` is the only
  writer) and `Global.restore_mouse_mode()` — every window still forces
  VISIBLE on open, but all now call `restore_mouse_mode()` on close instead
  of hardcoding a mode.

**Fullscreen resolution / camera distortion** (`project.godot`)
- Was launching windowed at a hardcoded 1280×1024 with `window/stretch/
  aspect="ignore"` (non-uniform stretch — the actual source of the reported
  camera distortion). Now `window/size/mode=3` (borderless fullscreen at
  the OS-reported native resolution, not hardcoded), 1920×1080 design
  base, `stretch/mode="canvas_items"` (keeps the 3D scene rendering at full
  native resolution instead of upscaling a fixed-size buffer),
  `stretch/aspect="keep_height"` (fills widescreen monitors instead of
  letterboxing, no non-uniform stretch).

**Night was pitch black** (`day_night_cycle.gd`)
- `moon_energy` (the sun light repurposed as moonlight at night) was 0.04 —
  about 3% of the daytime peak of 1.3. `night_ambient_energy` was 0.05,
  about 5% of daytime's 1.0. Both raised to 0.25 and 0.18 respectively —
  still a clearly dim, moody night, but no longer unable to make out
  terrain. Local light sources (campfires, torches) remain the dominant
  light source at night by design (see the TIME SYSTEM section in
  game_flow.txt) — this only fixes the ambient/moonlight floor.

**Camera look was one clamped mode, tied to F12** (`camera_controller.gd`)
- F12 mouselook was clamped to ±90°/±45° — fine for a "turn your head"
  feel, wrong for a free-look camera (couldn't even look directly behind
  you). Split into two distinct, independently-triggered modes: F12
  mouselook is now uncapped yaw (wraps via `wrapf`, doesn't grow unbounded)
  with pitch capped only to `±89°` (avoids flipping past vertical); holding
  Right Mouse Button (new, independent of F12) reuses the old ±90°/±45°
  clamp for a "head turn" feel, snapping to nothing special on release — it
  just stops moving. Mouse mode: RMB-held captures the cursor directly
  (bypassing `Global.mouselook_enabled`, which is reserved for the F12
  flag) and calls `Global.restore_mouse_mode()` on release.

**"You assume the Dusk Walker." missing the word "stance"** (`stance_bar.gd`)
- One-line format-string fix in `_on_slot_clicked()` — now
  `"You assume the %s stance."`, works for any current or future stance
  without needing "stance" baked into each `class_stances.json` entry's name.

### New Content

**Campfire warmth buff** (`campfire.gd`)
- `WarmthArea` (`Area3D`, already existed, previously just logged a flavor
  line) now starts a real `Timer` (30s, cancelled on early exit) when the
  player enters; on completion, grants "Warmth of the Campfire" via
  `combat_node.apply_effect("campfire_warmth", 300.0, {"hp_regen_bonus":2,
  "mana_regen_bonus":2, "stamina_regen_bonus":2})` — same `active_effects`
  system as stances, so it shows up in the new buff bar automatically.
  `player3d.gd`'s HP/mana regen tick (`_process()`) and stamina's continuous
  regen (`update_stamina()`) both now add
  `combat_node.get_modifier("*_regen_bonus")` alongside their existing
  calculations. Display name added to `SPELL_DISPLAY_NAMES`.

**Guard flavor text + scripted day/night conversations** (`guard_npc.gd`,
new `Scripts/npc_flavor_text.gd`, `Scripts/npc_scripted_conversation.gd`,
`Data/guard_flavor_text.json` restructured to `{hail:[...], engage:[...]}`,
new `Data/guard_conversations.json`)
- `NPCFlavorText`: reusable random-line-per-category picker (any future NPC
  just needs its own JSON file). Guards now have an `engage` line (fires
  once, on the IDLE→ENGAGE transition) alongside the existing `hail` lines.
- `NPCScriptedConversation`: reusable "pick two members of a group, play a
  random multi-line back-and-forth between them" component — replaces the
  old per-guard day/night line (both guards independently commenting was
  the actual bug being fixed: only one scripted 2-3 line exchange now plays
  per day/night transition, between two randomly-picked guards, from 4
  hand-written exchanges each in `guard_conversations.json`). Guards were
  also both renamed ("Guard Corwin"/"Guard Reyna" in
  `lumora_outskirts3d.tscn`) so the exchange reads as two different people.

---

## 2026-08-25 — Session 25: Archery target + bone spear set-dressing

### New Features

**Zone decoration** (`lumora_outskirts3d.tscn`)
- Added 3 "Archery Target" and 4 "Bone Spear" instances (`models/Archery
  Target.glb`, `models/Bone Spear.glb`) as decorative set-dressing around
  Lumora Outskirts — purely visual, no script attached, same grouping
  convention as the existing Campfires node.

## 2026-08-25 — Session 24: Guards targetable, detachable Combat log window

### Bug Fixes

**Lumora Guards couldn't be targeted at all**
- `tab_cycle_target()` and `_try_click_target()` (`player3d.gd`) only ever
  considered the `"monsters"` group and the `Monster` type — guards live in
  `"npc_guard"` as a plain `GuardNPC` (not a `Monster`), so Tab and click
  targeting silently skipped them entirely. Both now also pull from
  `"npc_guard"`, using a new `_is_targetable_alive()` helper that falls back
  to "always alive" for anything without a `current_state`/`State.DEAD`
  (guards have no death state at all).
- Making guards targetable exposed several latent crashes in code that
  assumed every target was a `Monster` (`.get_monster_name()`,
  `.State.DEAD`, `current_health`/`max_health`) — none of which exist on
  `GuardNPC`. Fixed by reusing/generalizing `TargetFrame.display_name()`
  (already static from the appraisal work) everywhere a target's name is
  needed, and adding a `combat_node`-or-fallback HP lookup in
  `_announce_target()`. `GuardNPC` also gained a top-level `level` var
  (mirroring `Monster`'s pattern) so the target frame and appraisal system
  show its real level instead of defaulting to 1.
- Since guards have no death handling (see Session 22 — one-directional,
  monsters can't hurt them, and nothing ever gave them a `die()`), attacking
  one would reduce its HP into a dead-end with no visible effect. Rather than
  build out guard death for an edge case nobody asked for, `attack_current_target()`,
  `cast_spell()`'s enemy branch, and `perform_melee_attack()` now all refuse to
  target anything `TargetFrame.faction_status()` reports as "Ally" — you
  simply can't attack your own guards, which also happens to be the sane
  default anyway.
- `_faction_reputation_text()` (appraisal) now also checks `npc_faction`
  (what `GuardNPC` actually exposes) as a fallback when `faction` isn't
  present, so appraising a guard shows its real standing ("Wardens of the
  Sacred Flame", Revered) instead of "Unaligned".

### New Features

**Detachable Combat log window** (`game_log_window.gd`)
- Small "Detach" button in the corner of the Combat tab pops the Combat log out
  into its own independent, draggable, resizable `CanvasLayer` window (same
  drag-bar/resize-handle/right-click-font-menu pattern as the main log
  window), so General and Combat can be arranged side by side instead of
  tab-switching between them.
- Implementation reparents the existing `CombatLog` `RichTextLabel` node
  itself (`Node.reparent()`) rather than copying text — all history and
  formatting carry over untouched, and `_on_combat()` keeps working
  unmodified since it's still the same node reference regardless of which
  window it currently lives in. The "Combat" tab is removed from the
  `TabContainer` while detached (`tabs.remove_child(combat_tab)`) and
  restored on reattach via a "Reattach" button in the detached window's title bar.
- Detached state and the detached window's position both persist to
  `ui_positions` (`chat_combat_detached`, `chat_combat`) and are restored on
  load, same as the main window's position/font size.

## 2026-08-25 — Session 22: Voidknight spell/stance revisions, guard combat, mob appraisal system

### Bug Fixes

**Voidknight started with a Blademaster scroll, not one of their own spells**
- `character_creation.gd`'s starting-scroll table gave every new Voidknight
  `scroll_of_power_strike` (a Blademaster spell) instead of any Voidknight
  spell — a fresh Voidknight had no way to ever learn their own kit. Fixed to
  grant `scroll_of_life_siphon` + `scroll_of_shadow_aura` (their two level-1
  spells). Also: **no scrolls existed at all** for any of the six Voidknight
  spells added last session — added `scroll_of_<spell_name>` items for all
  seven level 1-3 spells (including the new `shadowlight`) so they're
  actually learnable, matching the existing `scroll_of_power_strike` pattern.

### New Features

**Voidknight spell/stance revisions** (`player3d.gd:cast_spell()`,
`Data/class_stances.json`)
- `life_siphon` heal is now randomized 30-70% of damage dealt (was flat 50%).
- `shadow_aura` (display name "Aura of the Shadow") duration extended to 15
  minutes (was 30s).
- `improved_block` is now a **passive** — no longer castable/slotted. Added
  `CombatNode.has_passive(spell_name)` (checks the owner's `known_spells` via
  `get_parent()`, same lookup pattern as the existing stagger-attacker code)
  so its +10% block chance and +5% stagger chance apply automatically
  whenever it's known, with no `apply_effect()` call needed.
- New spell `shadowlight` (self, 1hr violet-light buff + flavor; "reveals
  hidden undead" is flavor only for now — no monster anywhere has a
  stealth/hidden state to detect).
- `spectral_minion` renamed in display to "Morthan's Call"; the summoned
  creature's own name changed to "Skeleton Warrior" to match. Added
  `Player3D.spell_display_name()` (static, with a small override table) since
  "Aura of the Shadow"/"Morthan's Call" aren't derivable from the internal
  snake_case spell_name the way the UI normally builds labels — used
  everywhere a spell name is shown (`cast_spell()`'s messages, the action bar).
- Stances fully replaced: **Shadow Ascendant** (+15% dmg, -10% block, -35%
  threat), **Necrotic Bastion** (+15% block, -10% dmg, +25% Life Drain
  healing), **Dusk Walker** (+7% dmg, +7% block). Added `threat_mult` (hooked
  into `CombatNode.generate_threat()`) and `life_drain_heal_mult` (hooked
  into `life_siphon`'s heal calc) to the buff/debuff engine.
  Note: `generate_threat()` itself is not called from anywhere else in the
  codebase yet (no aggro/threat-based targeting exists) — the threat_mult
  hook is correctly wired but has no visible gameplay effect until a
  threat-based system exists to consume it.

**Guards actively engage enemies within 4m** (`guard_npc.gd`)
- Guards now have a real `CombatNode` (stats from a new `lumora_guard` entry
  in `monsters.json`) and engage the nearest non-dead monster within 4m of
  their post, chasing up to a 6m leash before disengaging and returning home.
- One-directional: monsters don't fight back, since `monster3d.gd`'s entire
  state machine hardcodes "the player" as the only possible target — teaching
  monsters to recognize a second target type is out of scope for this pass
  (same limitation already noted for the pet). Guards are effectively
  invulnerable as a result.
- Guard kills are quiet: `Monster.die()` gained two optional params,
  `die(award_xp: bool = true, drop_loot: bool = true)` — guards call
  `die(false, false)`, skipping loot rolling and the 60s lootable-corpse
  window (instant `queue_free()`) and skipping the XP/level-up grant, since
  the player had no part in a kill their guard made unprompted.

**Mob Appraisal System** (`player3d.gd`, `target_frame.gd`)
- Automatic (shown the moment you target something, no roll): target level
  is now con-colored too (previously only the name was), reusing the
  existing 7-tier `_con_color()` scheme unchanged — deliberately did not
  introduce a second color scheme. New Ally/Neutral/Enemy label, derived from
  what already exists rather than new faction infrastructure: `npc_guard`
  group → Ally, `behavior_type == "passive"` → Neutral, everything else in
  the `monsters` group → Enemy. Boss mobs (`is_boss`) always show the most
  dangerous existing tier regardless of level.
- **Insight Check**: `I` key or `/appraise`, requires a current target, 10s
  cooldown per target. Roll: `d20 + max(INT mod, WIS mod)` vs a DC from level
  difference (5+ below→5, 2-4 below→10, ±1→15, 2-4 above→20, 5+ above→25) —
  a new, self-contained dice paradigm separate from the rest of combat's
  percentile rolls. Ability modifier is the standard `(stat-10)/2`.
  Success reveals difficulty flavor text, faction-modifier text, estimated
  threat, resistances/weaknesses, special ability, a loot-quality estimate
  (heuristic from monster level, not new per-item data), and exact faction
  reputation (standing number + Hated/Disliked/Neutral/Liked/Revered label).
  Failure/critical-failure temporarily show the wrong nameplate color (10s /
  30s) via new `TargetFrame.show_wrong_color()`, then self-correct. Critical
  success (nat 20) adds one extra line from an optional `secret_note` field.
- New optional `monsters.json` fields (all default-omitted, no existing
  entries required to change): `resistances`/`weaknesses` (display-only —
  deliberately not wired into `modify_damage()`, which stays hardcoded
  per-subclass; populated skeleton's to match its real `modify_damage()`
  values so at least one is accurate), `special_ability`, `is_boss`,
  `secret_note`, `corruption`.

### Bug Fixes

**Learned scrolls stayed visible in the backpack until an unrelated inventory action forced a repaint**
- Root cause: `Inventory.remove_from_basic_inventory()` and `remove_from_bag()`
  (`inventory_autoload.gd`) mutated inventory data and called `sync_to_global()`
  but never emitted `inventory_changed` — the only signal `backpack_ui.gd`
  listens for to repaint its slot grid. The scroll was actually gone from data
  immediately; it just stayed on screen until something else (equip, move,
  reopening the window) happened to trigger a redraw.
- Fixed by adding `inventory_changed.emit()` to both functions, matching the
  pattern already used by `equip_item()`, `unequip_item()`, and
  `move_item_between_slots()`.

### New Features

**Guard NPC + hail (H key / `/hail` chat command)**
- The old hail system (`npc.gd` + `chat_window.gd`, 2D `Area2D`-based) turned
  out to be fully dead code — only reachable from `Scenes/main.tscn`, which
  isn't the game's boot scene (`main_menu.tscn` is). There was no live 3D NPC
  of any kind before this.
- Added `Scripts/guard_npc.gd` + `Scenes/guard_npc.tscn` — a stationary guard
  that responds to a hail with a random line from `Data/guard_flavor_text.json`.
  Two instances ("Lumora Guard", "Lumora Sentry") placed in
  `lumora_outskirts3d.tscn` at the existing "Left Guard"/"Right Guard" statue
  locations.
- Simplified from the old 2D design: proximity-only (no facing-direction
  raycast requirement) — the old check leaned on 2D sprite-facing that doesn't
  translate to the free-look 3D camera.
- `H` (existing `hail` input action, previously only consumed by the dead 2D
  code) and a new `/hail` chat command both call
  `player3d.gd:try_hail_nearby_npc()`, which finds the nearest guard within 5m.

**Generic buff/debuff engine on `CombatNode`**
- Added `active_effects` dict + `apply_effect()` / `remove_effect()` /
  `has_effect()` / `get_modifier()` / a ticking `_process()` to `combatnode.gd`.
  Shared by the player and every `Monster` (both already own a `CombatNode`
  child), so it covers self-buffs, enemy debuffs, and (later) DoTs with one
  system instead of one-off hacks per spell.
- Hooked into `calculate_hit_chance()` (accuracy debuffs), `get_block_chance()`
  (block buffs), `calculate_melee_damage()` / `calculate_spell_damage()`
  (damage multipliers), a new stagger check in `resolve_attack()`'s BLOCK
  branch, and `monster3d.gd`'s `handle_movement()` / `perform_attack()`
  (movement/attack-speed slows).

**Voidknight spells 1-3 given real effects** (`player3d.gd:cast_spell()`)
- `life_siphon` — now heals the caster for 50% of the damage dealt.
- `shadow_aura` — 30s self-buff; continuously applies a short accuracy debuff
  to any monster within 10m.
- `blood_ritual` — sacrifices 10% current HP for a 10% damage buff, 8s.
- `necrotic_grasp` — 15% movement/attack-speed slow on the current target, 6s.
- `improved_block` — adapted to the existing all-or-nothing block model as
  +10% block chance (no partial-damage-absorption mechanic exists to hook
  "absorbs more damage" into) plus a literal 5% chance to stagger the attacker
  on a successful block.
- `spectral_minion` — see pet section below.
- Previously these all either did nothing (self/group-target spells only
  printed flavor text) or were undefined in code despite being fully specified
  in `player_spells.json`.

**Spectral Minion pet + command UI**
- New `Scripts/pet_minion.gd` + `Scenes/pet_minion.tscn` — deliberately *not*
  a `Monster` subclass, since `monster3d.gd`'s state machine is hardcoded to
  chase/attack the player. Independent actor with its own small state machine
  (Follow / Attack / Stop / Sit / Guard), stats from `monsters.json`'s
  `"skeleton"` entry with HP set to 40% of the caster's max HP.
- New `Scripts/pet_frame.gd` + `Scenes/pet_frame.tscn` — small draggable HUD
  panel with the pet's HP bar and six command buttons (Attack / Stop / Back /
  Follow / Sit / Guard). Spawned when `spectral_minion` is cast, freed when
  the pet dies.
- Known limitation: `lumora_outskirts3d.tscn`'s baked `NavigationRegion3D`
  navmesh currently has zero polygons (confirmed via `git show` — a real bake
  existed in an earlier commit but isn't in the current scene file), so the
  pet's pathing is the same straight-line-to-target behavior existing monsters
  already have on this empty nav map, not full pathfinding. Re-baking the
  navmesh is a separate fix.
- Also: nothing currently damages the pet, since every monster's AI only ever
  targets the player (no per-monster "who is attacking me" concept exists) —
  `apply_damage()`/`die()` are wired up on the pet for whenever that changes.

**Stance bar (Voidknight)**
- New `Data/class_stances.json`, `Scripts/stance_bar.gd` +
  `Scenes/stance_bar.tscn`. Small bar of stance slots shown above the action
  bar, indented right ("tabbed in"); hidden entirely for any class with no
  stances defined. Voidknight gets two: Aggressive (+10% damage, -5% block)
  and Defensive (-10% damage, +10% block), mutually exclusive, toggled via the
  buff/debuff engine above.

## 2026-08-25 — Session 23: Real threat/aggro system (makes threat_mult work)

### New Features

**Per-monster aggro table** (`monster3d.gd`)
- Session 22 wired `threat_mult` into `CombatNode.generate_threat()`, but
  nothing called `generate_threat()` at all and every monster hardcoded
  "the player" as its only possible attack target — so the stance modifier
  had nowhere to have an effect. Added a real (if intentionally small) aggro
  system to fix that:
  - `Monster.aggro_table: Dictionary` (attacker Node → accumulated threat
    float), `add_threat(attacker, amount)`, `get_current_target()` (returns
    whoever has the highest threat, falling back to the player if the table
    is empty).
  - `state_chase()`/`state_attack()`/`perform_attack()` now chase/attack
    `get_current_target()` instead of the hardcoded `player` reference.
    Initial engagement is unchanged — `can_see_player()`'s proximity check
    still decides whether a monster notices the player and starts fighting
    at all; the aggro table only decides *who* it keeps attacking once
    already in combat, which is exactly the decision `threat_mult` needed to
    influence.
  - Aggro table clears when a monster leashes back out of combat (loses the
    fight entirely), so old threat doesn't linger into the next encounter.
  - `perform_attack()` now handles hitting a non-player target: reuses the
    existing `CombatLogFormatter.monster_attack()` "hits you" messages only
    when the target actually is the player, and a new
    `_log_attack_on_other()` for third-person messages ("Skeleton hits
    Skeleton Warrior for 12 damage!") when it isn't.
- Threat is generated (and added to the target's `aggro_table`) at every
  place the player or pet deals damage or heals: `player3d.gd`'s
  `attack_current_target()` and `cast_spell()`'s enemy-damage branch
  (including a healing-threat bump for `life_siphon`'s self-heal, so Necrotic
  Bastion's "+25% Life Drain healing, maintains threat through healing"
  actually keeps the Voidknight on top of the aggro table), and
  `pet_minion.gd`'s `_perform_attack()`. Guards are deliberately excluded —
  monsters still can't target them at all (unchanged from Session 22's
  one-directional guard combat), so they never appear in any aggro table.
- Net effect: if a Voidknight in Shadow Ascendant (-35% threat) fights
  alongside their Morthan's Call pet, the pet generates relatively more
  threat and monsters will actually turn and attack it instead — matching
  the stance's "DPS/solo, not recommended for group tanking" design intent.
  Necrotic Bastion's healing-boosted threat has the opposite effect, keeping
  aggro on the Voidknight as intended for tanking.
- Verified with a standalone algorithm test (aggro-table max-threat selection
  logic + the real `CombatNode.generate_threat()`/`threat_mult` path) since
  `monster3d.gd` can't be loaded in isolation outside the full game (it
  references the `GameLog` autoload, unavailable to a bare headless test
  script) — the full-project `--check-only` pass confirms it compiles
  correctly in the real game.

## 2026-08-23 — Session 20: 3D level collision rebuild, chat/log overhaul, campfire audio, camera clamps, autorun

### Bug Fixes

**3D level collision regenerated from the new Blender export (Scenes/lumora_outskirts3d.tscn)**
- The zone's wall/floor collision was three hand-baked ConcavePolygonShape3D shapes
  frozen from the old 9.5KB placeholder geometry — completely decoupled from the
  actual level mesh, and totally stale after the Blender level art pass (glb grew
  to 112MB+). Built a headless Godot pipeline (`godot --headless --script ...`) that
  walks every MeshInstance3D in the imported .glb, generates a trimesh
  StaticBody3D/CollisionShape3D per mesh with the mesh's true world transform baked
  in, and text-splices the result into the .tscn without touching anything else
  (UIDs, unrelated nodes) — safer than a full PackedScene resave, which was tried
  first and silently dropped resource UIDs and failed to persist edits inside the
  instanced sub-scene at all.
- Re-run twice more as the source Blender file changed (texture size pass, then the
  Floor scale fix below) — collision now regenerates cleanly any time the .glb is
  re-exported.

**Root cause of "character falls through the world" / broken camera / "missing" sand texture**
- All three symptoms traced to one cause: Blender's `Floor` object had its scale on
  Blender's Z axis (which maps to Godot's Y/up axis on glTF export) set to exactly 0.
  Invisible in Blender since the mesh's local vertices already sit at Y=0, but it
  made the object's transform matrix singular. Godot's physics engine threw
  `Condition "det == 0"` trying to invert it every physics tick, corrupting the
  contact normal, which corrupted the player's CharacterBody3D transform — freezing
  movement and breaking the camera (parented to the corrupted transform), which read
  as "nothing is rendering right," not a texture problem.
- Fixed at the Blender source: `Floor` scale Z 0→1. Also caught and reverted a
  `location.z` of 1.0 left over from a first fix attempt (that axis maps to Godot Y
  and was floating the floor 1m above its correct height) back to 0.
- Diagnosed by inspecting the live working .blend directly with
  `blender --background --python` (found via `find -newer`, since the copy tracked
  in `zones/` was stale — the real source lives in a separate working folder) rather
  than guessing from the exported geometry.

**Duplicate "Docks" object removed from the Blender source** — collision mesh count
went from 15 to 14; confirmed intentional, not an accidental deletion.

**Campfire ambient sound not looping (Assets/…campfire…mp3.import)**
- `AudioStreamPlayer3D` (autoplay, max_distance=10.0 for the ~10m audible range) was
  already wired up correctly, but the imported MP3 had `loop=false` — it played once
  on scene load and then went silent forever, even if you walked back into range.
  Fixed: `loop=true`.

### New Features

**General-purpose chat input (Scenes/game_log_window.tscn, Scripts/game_log_window.gd)**
- The always-on "Chat" log window (General/Combat tabs) was output-only — no way to
  type anything outside of NPC dialogue. Added a LineEdit at the bottom; Enter
  submits. Non-slash text posts as "You say, '...'" to the General tab; `/`-prefixed
  text routes to a command handler.
- `/loc` command added here (prints player X/Y/Z) and also to the NPC dialogue chat
  box (chat_window.gd) where it was first requested.

**Local-time timestamps on all chat/log messages (game_log_window.gd)**
- `_append()` now prefixes every General and Combat line with a `[HH:MM AM/PM]`
  local-time stamp, applied centrally so it covers all existing log traffic
  (combat, quest text, system messages), not just new chat lines.

**Movement/combat input suppressed while typing (player3d.gd)**
- Since chat is now usable while walking around (not just static NPC dialogue),
  typing "w/a/s/d" or other bound letters into the chat box was also moving the
  character and firing combat actions. `_physics_process` now checks
  `get_viewport().gui_get_focus_owner() is LineEdit` and skips
  movement/jump/crouch/run-toggle/combat handling while any text field has focus,
  smoothly decelerating existing velocity to a stop instead of leaving it stale.

**Autorun (player3d.gd, project.godot)**
- New `toggle_autorun` input action. Holding forward is simulated via
  `autorun_enabled` in `handle_movement()`'s forward calculation; turning still
  works normally while autorunning. Cancels on `move_backward` or toggling again.
  Logs "Autorun enabled/disabled." to the General tab.
- Originally bound to tilde/backtick per request; discovered during a later check
  that tilde was already bound to `attack_target` (autoattack toggle) — my initial
  "is it free?" check only searched for text key names and missed the numeric
  keycode binding. Rebound to `R`, which is genuinely unused.

**Camera mouse-look clamps widened and corrected (camera_controller.gd)**
- The file's own doc comment claimed mouse-look was "active only while right mouse
  button is held," but the code actually implemented an F12 toggle — comment and
  implementation had drifted apart. Also: yaw (left/right) had no clamp at all, and
  pitch (up/down) was clamped to ±60° (120° total).
- Tried switching the trigger to right-mouse-hold as requested, but that conflicts
  with the existing right-click "loot corpse" binding, so reverted to F12 per
  follow-up request. Kept the clamp fixes: yaw now ±90° (180° total), pitch now
  ±45° (90° total). Comment corrected to match the code.

**Stamina regenerates while walking, not just standing/sitting (player3d.gd)**
- Previously walking (moving, not running) neither drained nor regenerated stamina
  at all — a dead zone. Added `STAMINA_REGEN_WALK = 5.0`. Full hierarchy: sitting
  20/s (fastest) > standing 10/s > walking 5/s (slowest, still nonzero) > running
  drains at 10/s.

**Procedurally generated sky** — added via WorldEnvironment (Background = Sky,
ProceduralSkyMaterial); guided setup, implemented in-editor.

### Notes / Known Gaps
- Texture size pass: `dense_sand`, `worn_planks`, `plastered_stone_wall`, and
  `marble_rock_02` now embed at 1024×1024 (down from 2048). The pool water texture
  is still 8192×8192 (16.5MB) — largest single texture in the zone, worth revisiting.
- Reimporting a re-exported .glb does **not** overwrite already-extracted loose
  texture files of the same name in `zones/` — delete stale ones before reimporting
  or Godot silently keeps using the old resolution.
- `melee_attack` input action exists but has no key bound.
- Godot's editor "Embed Game" toggle (not a project setting) was the likely cause
  of the game rendering as a small box even when the window was maximized —
  toggle it off near the Play buttons or in Editor Settings → Run → Window
  Placement if that resurfaces.

### Follow-up Fixes (same session)

**Tab targeting fixed (project.godot)**
- `tab_target` (the action actually polled by `handle_combat()` to cycle target)
  and `target_closest` (unused elsewhere in code) had their key bindings swapped
  — `tab_target` was on Escape, `target_closest` was on Tab. Swapped back:
  `tab_target` → Tab, `target_closest` → Escape. Escape's hardcoded pause-menu
  behavior in player3d.gd is unaffected (separate code path).
- `target_closest` then moved again, twice, to avoid new overlaps: Escape → Shift+Tab
  (conflicted with `toggle_run`, which fires on bare Shift key-down regardless of
  what else is held) → final: **Ctrl+Tab**.

**Ctrl+Escape clears target (player3d.gd, project.godot)**
- New `clear_target` input action (Ctrl+Escape) — added per request so a
  misclick/wrong target can be cleared without waiting out a Tab-cycle.
  `clear_target()` nulls `current_target`, resets the target frame, and turns
  off autoattack.
- The hardcoded `event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE` check in `_unhandled_input`
  (separate from the InputMap, opens the pause menu) would otherwise also fire
  on Ctrl+Escape — guarded with `and not event.ctrl_pressed` so Ctrl+Escape is
  exclusively the untarget action and plain Escape is exclusively the pause menu.

**"Empty corpse" feedback on right-click loot (player3d.gd)**
- Previously, right-clicking a DEAD monster with no loot (empty pending_loot
  from `roll_loot()`, or already fully looted) was indistinguishable from
  right-clicking empty air — both silently no-opped, since `_try_loot_corpse()`
  only ever considered monsters with `is_lootable == true` when picking a
  "nearest" target. Reported as "right-click looting stopped working" before
  being traced to this — the corpse just didn't have anything to drop.
- Fixed: nearest-DEAD-monster search no longer filters on `is_lootable`. If the
  nearest corpse in range has loot, opens the loot window as before; if not,
  logs "You search the corpse but find nothing upon it." to the General tab.
  The debug-console-only "No lootable corpse within range." warning is now
  reserved for the case where there's no corpse in range at all.

**Campfire ambient flavor text (Scripts/campfire.gd, Scenes/campfire.tscn)**
- Added a `WarmthArea` (Area3D, SphereShape3D radius 4.0) as a child of the
  campfire scene root, with a new `campfire.gd` script watching
  `body_entered`/`body_exited` for group `"player"`.
- On entry, logs "The warmth of the fire renews your spirit as you take
  shelter nearby." to the General tab via `GameLog.log_general()`. Fires once
  per approach (`_player_near` guard) and resets on exit so it fires again on
  a later visit, rather than spamming every physics frame spent near the fire.

## 2026-06-09 — Session 17: Spell casting — damage routing, grammar, combat log messages

### Bug Fixes

**Physical spells now use correct damage calculation (player3d.gd)**
- power_strike (school: physical) was using calculate_spell_damage() which adds
  INT-based arcane power — wrong for a combat ability.
- Physical school: base_damage + STR/2, mitigated by target AC, applied as physical
  (triggers monster resistances like skeleton's piercing resistance).
- Magical schools (arcane, fire, cold, etc.): use calculate_spell_damage() as before.

**Grammar fixed in CombatLogFormatter**
- "You casts Power Strike" → "You cast Power Strike"
- "You begins casting a spell." → "You begin casting a spell."
- Fixed across spell_damage, spell_heal, spell_debuff, begin_cast.

**Begin-cast message now actually logs (player3d.gd)**
- CombatLogFormatter.begin_cast("You") is now called before damage is applied,
  matching EQ-style "You begin casting a spell." appearing in the General tab.

**Spell cast improvements**
- spell_casting skill ticks on every successful cast.
- Battle Shout logs a colored group-cry message.
- Error messages improved: "not ready (Xs remaining)", "insufficient mana", etc.
- Mana is committed and cooldown starts even if target is invalid (you still cast).
  No: mana now rolls back gracefully — target checks happen before commit.

**Spell drag from abilities book to action bar (abilities_book.gd)**

  The action bar slots use set_drag_forwarding for drops, which expects drag data
  to come from _get_drag_data / set_drag_forwarding on the source — not force_drag.
- Fixed: _make_spell_row now uses set_drag_forwarding with a data provider lambda
  that returns {"type":"spell","name":...} and sets the drag preview.
  gui_input retained only for the hover highlight color change.

## 2026-06-09 — Session 11: Move mouse-look to F12 toggle, fix right-click loot

### Changes

**Mouse-look moved from right-click-hold to F12 toggle (camera_controller.gd)**
- Right-click-hold was capturing the mouse and centering it, conflicting with
  right-click looting of corpses.

  second press releases to free cursor.

**Right-click loot no longer requires MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED (player3d.gd)**

  works for looting in normal free-cursor mode.

  which was correct when right-click held mouse-look but wrong now.
  Changed to restore MOUSE_MODE_VISIBLE on close.

## 2026-06-09 — Session 10: Fix floating monsters, missing XP, missing loot

### Bug Fixes

**Monsters floating after spawn (monster3d.gd)**
- Gravity only ran inside handle_movement(), which only gets called in PATROL/CHASE
  states. Monsters in IDLE or ATTACK states had no gravity and floated in place.
- Fix: added an else branch that always applies gravity (velocity.y -= 20*delta)
  and calls move_and_slide() for all non-PATROL/CHASE states.

**No XP or loot awarded on kill (player3d.gd)**
- attack_current_target() (the autoattack path) calls combat_node.resolve_attack(),
  which subtracts HP directly on the CombatNode — bypassing monster3d.apply_damage().
  Because apply_damage() is what calls die(), the monster's die() method was never
  invoked: no XP award, no loot roll, no corpse despawn timer.
- perform_melee_attack() (manual click) already had target.die() — only autoattack
  was affected.
- Fix: save the target reference before clearing current_target, then call dead.die()
  after the death log and target clear. XP and loot now fire correctly on autoattack kills.

---

## 2026-06-09 — Session 9: Fix mob spawner crash (2D scenes in 3D spawner)

### Bug Fix

**mob_spawner3d.gd — crashed loading 2D monster scenes**
- Spawner was referencing rat.tscn, snake.tscn, etc. — all CharacterBody2D scenes using
  the legacy Mob base class. Instantiating them in the 3D world caused the
  "Could not resolve super class inheritance from Mob" error.
- Fix: spawner now always loads monster_template.tscn (a valid CharacterBody3D scene
  with NavigationAgent3D and CombatNode). Sets mob.monster_name = mob_type BEFORE
  calling add_child(), so Monster._ready() sees the name and loads the correct JSON
  stats from monsters.json automatically.
- Removed MOB_SCENES dict; replaced with MONSTER_TEMPLATE constant + VALID_MOB_TYPES
  validation list.

## 2026-06-09 — Session 8: Regen, target frame name/con, sit messages, skill-up message

### Bug Fixes

**Regeneration too slow (combatnode.gd + player3d.gd)**
- Old formula: 1 + int(con/5) = 3 HP/tick at CON 13. New: 2 + int(con/3) = 6 HP/tick.
- HP and mana regen now apply a 3× multiplier when the player is sitting.
- Troll racial "regeneration" trait now applied in apply_racial_modifiers(): +4 HP per
  regen tick, stacking with the sitting multiplier.
- Sitting Troll regen: (base 6 + racial 4) × 3 = 30 HP per 6-second tick.

**Target frame showing "Monster" instead of actual name (target_frame.gd)**
- _refresh_name_and_con() was calling get_monster_name() which returns the base-class
  default "monster" for any instance that doesn't override the virtual.
- Fixed: new _get_display_name() checks monster_description first (strips leading
  article "a"/"an"/"the" and capitalizes), falls back to monster_name var, then
  get_monster_name() as a last resort.
- _refresh_name_and_con() now runs every _process frame so con color updates live
  if the player levels up while a target is locked.

**Consider system thresholds corrected (target_frame.gd)**
- Red was diff >= 5; corrected to diff >= 6 (6+ levels above).
- Full system: Red ≥6, Orange ≥4, Yellow ≥2, White ≥-1, Blue ≥-3, Green ≥-5, Grey else.

### New Features

**Sit / stand log messages (player3d.gd)**
- Toggling sit now logs "You sit down to rest." / "You stand up." to the General tab.

**Skill-up message format (player3d.gd)**
- Changed from "Your [skill] skill has increased to X!" to
  "You've become better at [skill]! (X)"

## 2026-06-09 — Session 7: Reduce monster aggro/chase range to 5m

### Bug Fix / Tuning

**monster3d.gd — aggro and leash range capped at 5 meters**
- JSON aggro_range values are 2D-era units (100–200). Added AGGRO_RANGE_SCALE = 10.0
  (matches the speed / 10 conversion already in place) so ranges convert to meters.
- Added MAX_AGGRO_DISTANCE = 5.0 as a hard cap so no monster aggros beyond 5m regardless
  of what the JSON says.
- can_see_player() now uses minf(aggro_range / scale, 5.0) as the effective range,
  preserving passive (÷3) and neutral (÷2) behavior type modifiers.
- state_chase() leash check uses the same effective range so monsters stop chasing
  at the same distance they would have aggroed.

  Logs "Your [skill] skill has increased to X!" on gain.

**Combat log formatter (Scripts/combat_log_formatter.gd)**

- player_attack() / monster_attack(): randomized verb selection by damage type
  (slashing/piercing/blunt/generic), separate second/third person conjugation.

- spell_damage(), spell_heal(), spell_debuff(), begin_cast(), death() helpers.
- damage_type_from_item() maps item "skill" field → damage type string.
- monster3d.get_damage_type() derives damage type from monster category.
- All combat log calls in player3d.gd and monster3d.gd now route through formatter.

## 2026-06-08 — Session 4: Bug fixes — save/exit cleanup, loot despawn

### Bug Fixes

**Save and Exit — pause menu overlay persisted on main menu**
- `_on_save_and_exit()` was calling `change_scene_to_file()` without freeing the
  PauseMenu CanvasLayer first. Root-level nodes survive scene changes.
- Fix: added `queue_free()` before `change_scene_to_file()` in pause_menu.gd.

**Save and Exit — HUD (chat, action bar, frames) persisted on main menu**
- player_frame, target_frame, game_log_window, action_bar are all added to scene root
  in _spawn_hud() and survive `change_scene_to_file()`.
- Fix: all HUD nodes now tagged with group `"game_hud"` on spawn. `_on_save_and_exit()`
  iterates the group and queue_frees every node before changing scene.

**Monster — lootable infinitely after being looted**
- `corpse_loot_window.setup()` called `loot.duplicate(true)`, giving the window its own
  copy. Items taken from the window never depleted the monster's `pending_loot`, so
  `is_lootable` stayed true and the window could be reopened with full loot each time.
- Fix: removed `.duplicate(true)` — the window now holds a direct reference to the
  monster's array. `_take()` / `_loot_all()` mutate it in place.
- Added `signal all_looted` to corpse_loot_window, emitted when `_rebuild_list()` finds
  the array empty. `open_loot_window()` connects this to `_on_fully_looted()` which sets
  `is_lootable = false` and despawns the corpse after 5 seconds.
- Added `if not is_inside_tree(): return` guard in `die()`'s 60s auto-despawn await so
  it safely exits if the corpse was already freed via the loot path.

## 2026-06-08 — Session 2: Combat fixes, HUD improvements, action bar

### Bug Fixes

**combatnode.gd — `class_name` reserved keyword crash**
- `func set_class(class_name: String)` used a GDScript reserved keyword as a parameter.
- Renamed parameter to `new_class`. Game was unlaunchable until this fix.

**player_frame.gd — `get()` too many arguments**
- `_player.get("current_stamina", 0.0)` — GDScript's Object.get() takes 1 arg, not 2.
- Changed to ternary `_player.get("x") if "x" in _player else default`.

**combatnode.gd — ATK stat double-counted in damage**
- `calculate_melee_damage()` added `get_atk()` to raw damage.
- ATK is a hit-accuracy stat used in `calculate_hit_chance()`, not a damage bonus.
- Monsters had `gear_atk = 40 + level×10`; a level-2 skeleton was dealing 60+ base damage.
- Fix: removed `get_atk()` from damage formula. Damage is now `weapon_damage + str/2`.

**monster3d.gd — monster hit rate too high**
- `gear_atk = 40 + level×10` gave skeleton ~95% hit rate vs player AC 14.
- Changed to `gear_atk = 15 + level×5` (level-2 skeleton now ~55-60% hit rate).

**project.godot — A/D keys mapped to ui_left/ui_right**
- Caused TabContainer in game_log_window to switch tabs while strafing.
- Removed physical_keycode 65 (A) from ui_left and 68 (D) from ui_right.
- Arrow keys remain bound; A/D now only fire player movement actions.

**camera_controller.gd — mouse_mode conflict with Alt toggle**
- Removed Alt-key mouse toggle (unreliable).
- Replaced with right-click-hold for mouse look: MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED while held,
  MOUSE_MODE_VISIBLE on release. Default mode is now VISIBLE (cursor free).

---

### New Features

**Combat log formatting**
- Monster attacks: "A hooded bandit hits you for 12 damage!" (description from monsters.json)
- Player attacks: "You hit/critically hit a hooded bandit with melee attack for X damage!"
- Parry/block/dodge/riposte all have distinct messages.
- `monster_description` field loaded from monsters.json in monster3d.gd.

**Autoattack indicator**
- Flashing red dot (⬤) added to top-right of game_log_window panel.
- Animated via Tween (fade in/out). Appears when autoattack on, disappears on off/kill.
- Driven by `GameLog.autoattack_changed(bool)` signal + `GameLog.set_autoattack(bool)`.

**Action bar (Scenes/action_bar.tscn + Scripts/action_bar.gd)**
- 12 slots: keys 1–9, 0, -, =
- Bottom-center of screen, built entirely in _ready() (no layout in .tscn).
- Filled slots show gold border tint and spell name (word-wrapped).
- On-cooldown slots show darkened overlay + countdown timer in seconds.
- Reads `player.known_spells` and `player._spell_cooldowns` via polling in _process().

**Spell casting (player3d.gd)**
- `cast_spell(spell_name)` — checks cooldown, mana, applies damage via
  `combat_node.calculate_spell_damage()`, logs to combat tab.
- `_tick_cooldowns(delta)` decrements per-spell timers each physics frame.
- `_spell_by_name` dict built alongside existing `_spell_cache` for O(1) name lookup.
- Keys 1–9, 0, -, = fire `cast_spell(known_spells[i])` from `_unhandled_input`.

**Starting equipment + weapon skill**
- `apply_equipment(equipment: Dict)` reads items.json, sets weapon_damage + gear_ac on CombatNode.
- Starting weapon_skill set by class on first load: Blademaster/Voidknight/etc = 10,
  ranged/martial = 8, caster = 4.
- Both trigger `recalculate_derived_stats()` after application.

**Dev character fallback**
- When launching a 3D scene directly from the editor (Global.player_data empty),
  player3d.gd now loads hardcoded Blademaster dev defaults instead of erroring out.

### Deprecations / Cleanup

**Data/character_stats.json — archived**
- Was never read by any active script (only by character_sheet.gd.old).
- Moved to archive-2d/Data/character_stats.json.
- Dev defaults now live inline in player3d.load_player_data_from_global().

---

## 2026-06-08 — Godot Startup Crash Fix

### Problem
Game crashed on startup with:
  Error at (36, 16): Could not parse global class "CombatNode" from "res://Scripts/combatnode-OLD.gd"

### Root Cause
.godot/uid_cache.bin contained stale UID entries for scripts that had been deleted
from the Scripts/ directory (backpack_ui-OLD.gd, character_creation-OLD.gd,
inventory-OLD.gd). Godot tried to resolve these UIDs on startup and failed.

### Fix
Deleted stale cache files and let Godot rebuild them on next launch:
  - .godot/uid_cache.bin
  - .godot/global_script_class_cache.cfg

### Also
Committed archive-2d/ directory to git as a versioned backup of deprecated 2D
scripts and scenes (combatnode-OLD, mob-OLD, player-OLD, backpack_ui-OLD, etc.).
